- [ ] **Step 3: Add the item, the visibility and the icon's frame**

`Stickermine/App/StatusItemController.swift` (complete new version; it replaces the whole file):

```swift
import AppKit
import Carbon.HIToolbox

/// The menu bar icon (spec section 13): the pickaxe glyph from
/// `design/menubar-glyph.svg` (asset `MenuBarGlyph`, a template image drawn
/// white or black to match the menu bar). A left-click starts a capture; a
/// right-click or Control-click shows the menu: New Sticker (with the
/// shortcut; disabled during a session), Open Stickermine… and Quit. The
/// icon can be hidden (`showMenuBarIcon`); the item is kept and shown again.
/// `MenuBarExtra` isn't used: it can't act on a plain click.
@MainActor
final class StatusItemController: NSObject {
    static let glyphName = "MenuBarGlyph"
    /// The SVG reports 200 × 200 pt; the menu bar wants 18 × 18.
    static let glyphSize = NSSize(width: 18, height: 18)

    private let statusItem: NSStatusItem
    private let menu: NSMenu
    private let newStickerItem: NSMenuItem

    /// A left-click or the menu's New Sticker.
    var onCapture: (() -> Void)?
    /// The menu's Open Stickermine….
    var onOpenHome: (() -> Void)?
    /// The menu's Quit.
    var onQuit: (() -> Void)?

    /// While a session runs, New Sticker is disabled (spec section 5.2).
    var sessionActive = false {
        didSet { Self.applySessionState(sessionActive, to: newStickerItem) }
    }

    init(shortcut: Shortcut?) {
        statusItem = NSStatusBar.system.statusItem(withLength: NSStatusItem.squareLength)
        let built = Self.makeMenu(shortcut: shortcut)
        menu = built.menu
        newStickerItem = built.newSticker
        super.init()
        newStickerItem.target = self
        newStickerItem.action = #selector(newSticker(_:))
        built.openHome.target = self
        built.openHome.action = #selector(openHome(_:))
        built.quit.target = self
        built.quit.action = #selector(quit(_:))
        if let button = statusItem.button {
            button.image = Self.glyph()
            button.toolTip = "Stickermine"
            button.setAccessibilityLabel("Stickermine")
            button.target = self
            button.action = #selector(buttonClicked(_:))
            button.sendAction(on: [.leftMouseUp, .rightMouseUp])
        }
    }

    /// Shows the shortcut in the menu (nil: none).
    func setShortcut(_ shortcut: Shortcut?) {
        Self.applyShortcut(shortcut, to: newStickerItem)
    }

    /// "Show menu bar icon" (spec section 13).
    var isVisible: Bool {
        get { statusItem.isVisible }
        set { statusItem.isVisible = newValue }
    }

    /// The icon's frame on screen, for the support ask to hang below; nil
    /// while the icon is hidden.
    var buttonScreenFrame: CGRect? {
        guard statusItem.isVisible, let button = statusItem.button, let window = button.window else { return nil }
        return window.convertToScreen(button.convert(button.bounds, to: nil))
    }

    /// The glyph from the asset catalog, as an 18 × 18 pt template image.
    static func glyph() -> NSImage? {
        guard let named = NSImage(named: glyphName), let image = named.copy() as? NSImage else { return nil }
        image.size = glyphSize
        image.isTemplate = true
        return image
    }

    /// The menu: New Sticker (with the shortcut), Open Stickermine…, a
    /// separator, Quit Stickermine.
    static func makeMenu(shortcut: Shortcut?)
        -> (menu: NSMenu, newSticker: NSMenuItem, openHome: NSMenuItem, quit: NSMenuItem) {
        let menu = NSMenu()
        menu.autoenablesItems = false
        let newSticker = NSMenuItem(title: "New Sticker", action: nil, keyEquivalent: "")
        applyShortcut(shortcut, to: newSticker)
        menu.addItem(newSticker)
        let openHome = NSMenuItem(title: "Open Stickermine…", action: nil, keyEquivalent: "")
        menu.addItem(openHome)
        menu.addItem(.separator())
        let quit = NSMenuItem(title: "Quit Stickermine", action: nil, keyEquivalent: "q")
        quit.keyEquivalentModifierMask = [.command]
        menu.addItem(quit)
        return (menu, newSticker, openHome, quit)
    }

    /// The item shows `shortcut` as its key equivalent (nothing when there is
    /// none or the key can't be shown in a menu).
    static func applyShortcut(_ shortcut: Shortcut?, to item: NSMenuItem) {
        guard let shortcut, let key = shortcut.menuKeyEquivalent else {
            item.keyEquivalent = ""
            item.keyEquivalentModifierMask = []
            return
        }
        item.keyEquivalent = key
        item.keyEquivalentModifierMask = modifierFlags(carbon: shortcut.modifiers)
    }

    static func applySessionState(_ active: Bool, to item: NSMenuItem) {
        item.isEnabled = !active
    }

    /// A Carbon modifier mask as AppKit modifier flags.
    static func modifierFlags(carbon: UInt32) -> NSEvent.ModifierFlags {
        var flags: NSEvent.ModifierFlags = []
        if carbon & UInt32(controlKey) != 0 { flags.insert(.control) }
        if carbon & UInt32(optionKey) != 0 { flags.insert(.option) }
        if carbon & UInt32(shiftKey) != 0 { flags.insert(.shift) }
        if carbon & UInt32(cmdKey) != 0 { flags.insert(.command) }
        return flags
    }

    /// A right-click or Control-click opens the menu; any other click captures.
    static func opensMenu(type: NSEvent.EventType, modifiers: NSEvent.ModifierFlags) -> Bool {
        type == .rightMouseUp || type == .rightMouseDown || modifiers.contains(.control)
    }

    @objc private func buttonClicked(_ sender: Any?) {
        let event = NSApp.currentEvent
        if let event, Self.opensMenu(type: event.type, modifiers: event.modifierFlags) {
            showMenu()
        } else {
            onCapture?()
        }
    }

    private func showMenu() {
        statusItem.menu = menu
        statusItem.button?.performClick(nil)
        statusItem.menu = nil
    }

    @objc private func newSticker(_ sender: Any?) {
        onCapture?()
    }

    @objc private func openHome(_ sender: Any?) {
        onOpenHome?()
    }

    @objc private func quit(_ sender: Any?) {
        onQuit?()
    }
}
```

### Task 8: TutorialLayout: where the tutorial stickers go

**Files:**
- Create: `Stickermine/Onboarding/TutorialLayout.swift`
- Test: `StickermineTests/TutorialLayoutTests.swift` (create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StickerGeometry.boundingSize(of:rotation:)`, `StickerGeometry.pose(center:scale:in:)` (M2/M5), `Pose` (M2).
- Produces (pure, CoreGraphics only):

```text
enum TutorialLayout {
    static let margin: CGFloat                                            // 24
    static let gap: CGFloat                                               // 16, between the cards
    static let minimumColumn: CGFloat                                     // 200
    static let rotations: [Double]                                        // −2°, +1.5°, +3° in radians
    struct Item: Equatable { let rect: CGRect; let pose: Pose }           // the rotated box on screen; its pose
    static func homeFrame(size: CGSize, in visibleFrame: CGRect) -> CGRect   // Home's first frame
    static func homeSize(preferred: CGSize, minimum: CGSize, in visibleFrame: CGRect) -> CGSize   // Home's first size
    static func layout(naturalSizes: [CGSize], rotations: [Double] = rotations, frame: CGRect,
                       visibleFrame: CGRect, homeSize: CGSize) -> [Item]  // tutorial 1, 2, Maple
}
```

The exact positions the spec leaves to the plan: Home's first frame is its size centred on the main display's visible frame (whole points; `HomeWindowController` uses the same function, Task 13). That size is 640 × 640, narrowed (never below Home's 560 pt minimum width) so the column right of it keeps 200 pt plus its two 24 pt margins, and shortened (never below 480 pt) to fit the visible height with 24 pt above and below (`homeSize`; `HomeWindowController.show()` and the first run's `placeFirstRun` both use it, Tasks 13 and 14). On a 1024 × 640 "Larger Text" mode (visible 1024 × 607) Home starts at 560 × 559 and spans x 232–792, and the column is x 800–1000. The column for the tutorials runs from Home's right edge plus 24 pt to the visible frame's right edge minus 24 pt (widened leftwards to 200 pt when the visible frame is narrower than 1056 pt; the narrowed Home still stays clear of it down to 1008 pt). Each sticker, turned by its rotation, is scaled to fit within a third of the display's height and the column's width, and never up. All three are right-aligned on the column's right edge: tutorial 1's top is 24 pt below the visible frame's top, tutorial 2's top 16 pt below tutorial 1, Maple's bottom 24 pt above the visible frame's bottom. On a 1512 × 982 display with the menu bar (visible 1512 × 949) and Home at 640 × 640, Home spans x 436–1076, the column is x 1100–1488, and Maple (634 × 455 pt with her bubble) is scaled to 0.59, filling the column's 388 pt.

```swift
import CoreGraphics

@testable import Stickermine

struct TutorialLayoutTests {
    /// A 1512 × 982 built-in display with the menu bar (33 pt) and no Dock.
    private let frame = CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 1512, height: 982)

    private let sizes = [CGSize(width: 300, height: 128), CGSize(width: 330, height: 128), CGSize(width: 634, height: 455)]

    @Test func homeStartsCentredOnWholePoints() {
        #expect(TutorialLayout.homeFrame(size: home, in: visible) == CGRect(x: 436, y: 155, width: 640, height: 640))
        #expect(TutorialLayout.homeFrame(size: home, in: CGRect(x: 50_000, y: 80, width: 1512, height: 869))
                == CGRect(x: 50_436, y: 195, width: 640, height: 640))

    @Test func cardsStackTopRightAndMapleSitsBottomRight() {

        let right = visible.maxX - TutorialLayout.margin
        #expect(items.allSatisfy { near($0.rect.maxX, right) })
        #expect(near(items[0].rect.maxY, visible.maxY - TutorialLayout.margin))
        #expect(near(items[1].rect.maxY, items[0].rect.minY - TutorialLayout.gap))
        #expect(near(items[2].rect.minY, visible.minY + TutorialLayout.margin))
    }

    @Test func everyStickerFitsAThirdOfTheHeightAndTheColumnBesideHome() {
        let items = TutorialLayout.layout(naturalSizes: sizes, frame: frame, visibleFrame: visible, homeSize: home)
        let homeFrame = TutorialLayout.homeFrame(size: home, in: visible)
        for item in items {
            #expect(item.rect.height <= frame.height / 3 + 0.01)
            #expect(item.rect.minX >= homeFrame.maxX + TutorialLayout.margin - 0.01)
            #expect(!item.rect.intersects(homeFrame))
            #expect(visible.contains(item.rect))
            #expect(item.pose.scale <= 1)
        }
        #expect(!items[0].rect.intersects(items[1].rect))
        #expect(!items[1].rect.intersects(items[2].rect))
        // Maple (634 pt wide, turned 3°) is the one the column shrinks.

        #expect(near(items[2].rect.width, visible.maxX - TutorialLayout.margin - (homeFrame.maxX + TutorialLayout.margin)))
    }

    @Test func smallStickersAreNeverScaledUp() {
        let small = [CGSize(width: 100, height: 40), CGSize(width: 120, height: 40), CGSize(width: 90, height: 120)]
        let items = TutorialLayout.layout(naturalSizes: small, frame: frame, visibleFrame: visible, homeSize: home)
        #expect(items.allSatisfy { $0.pose.scale == 1 })
    }

    @Test func thePosesDrawExactlyWhereTheLayoutSays() {
        let display = Fixtures.display(uuid: Fixtures.mainUUID, id: 1, frame: CGRect(x: 50_000, y: 0, width: 1512, height: 982),
                                       visibleFrame: CGRect(x: 50_000, y: 0, width: 1512, height: 949))

                                          homeSize: home)

            #expect(drawn.map { near($0.stickerRect.width, item.rect.width) && near($0.stickerRect.height, item.rect.height) } == true)
        }
    }

    @Test func aNarrowDisplayStillGetsAUsableColumn() {
        // Home's full 640 pt here; the app narrows it on such a display (next test).
        let small = CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 1024, height: 768)

    @Test func aSmallDisplayGetsASmallerHomeThatTheTutorialsClear() {
        // 1024 × 640 "Larger Text" on a 13" MacBook Air, below the 33 pt menu bar.
        let small = CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 1024, height: 607)
        let minimum = CGSize(width: 560, height: 480)

        #expect(items.allSatisfy { !$0.rect.intersects(homeFrame) })
        // A display with room keeps Home's full size.
        #expect(TutorialLayout.homeSize(preferred: home, minimum: minimum, in: visible) == home)

    @Test func theRotationsStayWithinTheBrandRange() {
        #expect(TutorialLayout.rotations.count == 3)
        #expect(TutorialLayout.rotations.allSatisfy { $0 >= -4 * .pi / 180 && $0 <= 3 * .pi / 180 })
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the layout**

`Stickermine/Onboarding/TutorialLayout.swift` (complete file):

```swift
import CoreGraphics
import Foundation

/// Where the first-run tutorial stickers go (spec section 13). Pure.
///
/// On the main display, clear of Home's initial frame (centred in the
/// visible frame): the two text cards stacked in the top-right corner,
/// Maple in the bottom-right corner, all right-aligned in the column between
/// Home's right edge and the visible frame's right edge, `margin` from every
/// edge. Each sticker, turned by its small rotation (the brand's "slight
/// rotation between −4° and +3°"), is scaled down (never up) to fit within a
/// third of the display's height and the column's width. On a small display
/// Home starts smaller (`homeSize`) so the column keeps `minimumColumn`; a
/// column still narrower than that is widened to it, leftwards.
enum TutorialLayout {
    static let margin: CGFloat = 24
    static let gap: CGFloat = 16
    static let minimumColumn: CGFloat = 200
    /// Tutorial 1, 2 and Maple: −2°, +1.5°, +3° (radians, counter-clockwise).
    static let rotations: [Double] = [-2 * .pi / 180, 1.5 * .pi / 180, 3 * .pi / 180]

    struct Item: Equatable {
        /// The rotated sticker's box on screen.
        let rect: CGRect
        let pose: Pose
    }

    /// Home's first frame: `size` centred in `visibleFrame`, on whole points.
    static func homeFrame(size: CGSize, in visibleFrame: CGRect) -> CGRect {
        CGRect(x: (visibleFrame.midX - size.width / 2).rounded(), y: (visibleFrame.midY - size.height / 2).rounded(),
               width: size.width, height: size.height)
    }

    /// Home's first size on a display: `preferred`, narrowed (never below
    /// `minimum`) so the column right of it keeps `minimumColumn`, and
    /// shortened to fit `visibleFrame` with `margin` above and below.
    static func homeSize(preferred: CGSize, minimum: CGSize, in visibleFrame: CGRect) -> CGSize {
        let width = min(preferred.width, visibleFrame.width - 2 * (minimumColumn + 2 * margin))
        let height = min(preferred.height, visibleFrame.height - 2 * margin)
        return CGSize(width: max(minimum.width, width.rounded(.down)), height: max(minimum.height, height.rounded(.down)))
    }

    /// One item per natural size (tutorial 1, 2, Maple), with `rotations` in the same order.
    static func layout(naturalSizes: [CGSize], rotations: [Double] = rotations, frame: CGRect,
                       visibleFrame: CGRect, homeSize: CGSize) -> [Item] {
        precondition(naturalSizes.count == 3 && rotations.count == 3, "three tutorial stickers")
        let home = homeFrame(size: homeSize, in: visibleFrame)
        let right = visibleFrame.maxX - margin
        let column = max(right - (home.maxX + margin), minimumColumn)
        let maxHeight = frame.height / 3
        let boxes: [(size: CGSize, scale: Double)] = zip(naturalSizes, rotations).map { natural, rotation in
            let box = StickerGeometry.boundingSize(of: natural, rotation: rotation)
            guard box.width > 0, box.height > 0 else { return (.zero, 1) }
            let scale = min(1, Double(maxHeight / box.height), Double(column / box.width))
            return (CGSize(width: box.width * CGFloat(scale), height: box.height * CGFloat(scale)), scale)
        }
        let first = CGRect(x: right - boxes[0].size.width, y: visibleFrame.maxY - margin - boxes[0].size.height,
                           width: boxes[0].size.width, height: boxes[0].size.height)
        let second = CGRect(x: right - boxes[1].size.width, y: first.minY - gap - boxes[1].size.height,
                            width: boxes[1].size.width, height: boxes[1].size.height)
        let maple = CGRect(x: right - boxes[2].size.width, y: visibleFrame.minY + margin,
                           width: boxes[2].size.width, height: boxes[2].size.height)
        return zip([first, second, maple], boxes).map { rect, box in
            Item(rect: rect, pose: StickerGeometry.pose(center: CGPoint(x: rect.midX, y: rect.midY), scale: box.scale,
                                                         in: frame))
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 9: TutorialStickers: the artwork, the first run and tutorial 1's redraw

**Files:**
- Create: `Stickermine/Onboarding/TutorialStickers.swift`
- Modify: `Stickermine/Stickers/StickerManager.swift` (add `masterChanged(for:)`)
- Test: `StickermineTests/TutorialStickersTests.swift` (create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LibraryStore.tutorialIDs`, `writePNG(_:for:)`, `deletePNG(for:)`, `pngURL(for:)` (M2); `LibraryController.mutate`, `flush()`, `hasUnsavedChanges`, `isReadOnly`, `store` (M2); `StickerManager.recordKeyForUserEdit(on:)`, `reconcileNow()`, `remove(placement:)` (M2/M5; Remove already deletes a tutorial outright); `ScreenProvider.mainDisplay` (M2); `MasterImage.pngData(_:pixelsPerPoint:)`, `MasterImage.fileInfo(at:)` (M2/M4); `BundledImages.cgImage(named:)` and `BundledImages.mapleName` (M1: Maple is 620 × 910 px at 2 px/pt); `StyleRenderer.invalidateCache(for:)` (M3); `TutorialLayout` (Task 8); `UserDefaults.didFirstRun` (Task 1).
- Produces:

```text
@MainActor enum TutorialArt {
    static let pixelsPerPoint: CGFloat                                    // 2
    static let blue, mint, ink, pink: NSColor                             // #3D4BE0 #8FE3BE #1F2340 #FF4F81
    static let cardFontSize, cardRadius, cardBorder, lineGap, edge: CGFloat   // 24, 36, 10, 2, 1 pt
    static let cardPadding: CGSize                                        // 34 × 18 pt
    static let bubbleSize: CGSize; static let bubbleBorder, bubbleTop, bubbleOverlap, bubbleFontSize: CGFloat   // 322 × 44; 8, 100, 6, 18
    static let doubleClickText: String; static let bubbleText: String
    static func shortcutText(for shortcut: Shortcut?) -> String          // "Press ⌃⌥⌘S\nto make a sticker" / menu bar icon
    static func masters(shortcut: Shortcut?) -> [CGImage]?                // tutorial 1, 2, Maple
    static func shortcutCard(for shortcut: Shortcut?) -> CGImage?
    static func roundedFont(size: CGFloat, weight: NSFont.Weight) -> NSFont   // SF Pro Rounded
    static func card(text: String, fill: NSColor, textColor: NSColor) -> CGImage?
    static func mapleWithBubble(_ maple: CGImage) -> CGImage?
    static func bubblePath() -> CGPath                                    // the website's SVG path
}
@MainActor final class TutorialStickers {
    static let style: StickerStyle                                        // .shadow
    init(library: LibraryController, stickers: StickerManager, screens: ScreenProvider, defaults: UserDefaults)
    func placeFirstRun(shortcut: Shortcut?, homeSize: CGSize, completion: ((Bool) -> Void)? = nil)   // true: index saved
    func shortcutChanged(to shortcut: Shortcut?, completion: (() -> Void)? = nil)
}
extension StickerManager { func masterChanged(for sticker: UUID) }        // re-render even an unchanged request
```

How the first run goes: the three masters are drawn on the main actor (a few milliseconds), encoded, and written off the main actor (spec section 12: PNGs on a background queue); then on the main actor the three records (`tutorial: true`, Shadow, 2 px/pt) and their placements (Shadow, the layout's pose for the main display's size, the layout's rotation) are added in one `mutate`, the index is flushed, `didFirstRun` is set only when nothing is left unsaved, and the stickers are reconciled onto the screen. A failed PNG write deletes the PNGs and leaves `didFirstRun` false, so the next user launch tries again. After a shortcut change, tutorial 1's PNG is rewritten first and its record's pixel size updated afterwards (PNG before index); if tutorial 1 was removed meanwhile, the new PNG is deleted again so launch repair can't adopt it. `masterChanged(for:)` is needed because `StickerManager` only renders when the request changes, and a new shortcut can draw a card of the same size.

The tests take the fake main display away while the tutorials are saved, so no panel is ordered in across an `await` (the M2–M5 rule); the test that checks the panels puts the display back and closes them in the same synchronous stretch.

```swift
import AppKit

@testable import Stickermine

/// The first run against a temporary library and a fake main display at
/// x = 50 000. The display is taken away while the tutorials are saved, so no
/// panel is ordered in across an `await`; tests that need panels put it back
/// and close them in the same synchronous stretch.
@MainActor
struct TutorialStickersTests {
    /// Home's first size (`HomeWindowController.initialSize`).
    static let homeSize = CGSize(width: 640, height: 640)

    private func suite() -> (UserDefaults, String) {
        let name = "StickermineTests-\(UUID().uuidString)"
        return (UserDefaults(suiteName: name)!, name)
    }

    /// Premultiplied sRGB RGBA at (x, y) points from the top-left of a 2 px/pt master.
    private func pixel(_ image: CGImage, _ x: CGFloat, _ y: CGFloat) -> [UInt8] {
        TestImages.rgba(of: image, x: Int(x * 2), y: Int(y * 2))
    }

    private func near(_ rgba: [UInt8], _ expected: [UInt8], _ tolerance: Int = 3) -> Bool {
        zip(rgba, expected).allSatisfy { abs(Int($0) - Int($1)) <= tolerance }
    }

    private func firstRun(_ tutorials: TutorialStickers, screens: FakeScreens,
                          shortcut: Shortcut? = .defaultCapture) async -> Bool {
        await withCheckedContinuation { continuation in
            tutorials.placeFirstRun(shortcut: shortcut, homeSize: Self.homeSize) {
                continuation.resume(returning: $0)
            }
            screens.all = []
        }
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Let a changed master render again**

In `Stickermine/Stickers/StickerManager.swift`, insert the following directly above the declaration that starts with `func closeAllPanels() {` (above its `///` comment lines), followed by one blank line:

```swift
    /// A sticker's master changed under the same ID (tutorial 1 redrawn for a
    /// new shortcut): every placement of it is re-resolved from the model and
    /// rendered again, even when its render request is unchanged (a new
    /// shortcut can draw a card of the same size). Nothing is written.
    func masterChanged(for sticker: UUID) {
        renderer.invalidateCache(for: sticker)
        let model = library.library
        for (id, entry) in entries where model.placement(id)?.sticker == sticker {
            entry.layout = nil
            entry.request = nil
        }
        reconcileNow()
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the artwork and the first run**

`Stickermine/Onboarding/TutorialStickers.swift` (complete file):

```swift
import AppKit
import os

/// The tutorial stickers' artwork (spec section 13), drawn in code at 2 px
/// per point: two text cards in SF Pro Rounded on sticker-shaped cards in the
/// brand colours with their own 10 pt white border (the website's
/// description card), and Maple with the website's pink speech bubble beside
/// her mouth (#FF4F81, 8 pt white border, white SF Pro Rounded text). Their
/// borders are in the image, so they use the Shadow style.
@MainActor
enum TutorialArt {
    static let pixelsPerPoint: CGFloat = 2

    static let blue = NSColor(srgbRed: 0x3D / 255.0, green: 0x4B / 255.0, blue: 0xE0 / 255.0, alpha: 1)
    static let mint = NSColor(srgbRed: 0x8F / 255.0, green: 0xE3 / 255.0, blue: 0xBE / 255.0, alpha: 1)
    static let ink = NSColor(srgbRed: 0x1F / 255.0, green: 0x23 / 255.0, blue: 0x40 / 255.0, alpha: 1)
    static let pink = NSColor(srgbRed: 0xFF / 255.0, green: 0x4F / 255.0, blue: 0x81 / 255.0, alpha: 1)

    // Card metrics in points (the site's card: 10 px border, 36 px radius, 18 × 34 px padding).
    static let cardFontSize: CGFloat = 24
    static let cardPadding = CGSize(width: 34, height: 18)
    static let cardRadius: CGFloat = 36
    static let cardBorder: CGFloat = 10
    static let lineGap: CGFloat = 2
    /// Transparent edge around every master, like a cutout's crop padding.
    static let edge: CGFloat = 1

    // Maple and her bubble, in points from the top-left, as on the website:
    // a 322 × 44 bubble whose right edge sits 6 pt into Maple's box, its top
    // 100 pt below hers, its tail reaching down to y = 62.
    static let bubbleSize = CGSize(width: 322, height: 44)
    static let bubbleBorder: CGFloat = 8
    static let bubbleTop: CGFloat = 100
    static let bubbleOverlap: CGFloat = 6
    static let bubbleFontSize: CGFloat = 18

    static let doubleClickText = "Double-click me to move,\nresize or remove."
    static let bubbleText = "Make your desktop make you smile!"

    /// Tutorial 1's words: the current shortcut, or the menu bar icon when none is set.
    static func shortcutText(for shortcut: Shortcut?) -> String {
        guard let shortcut else { return "Click the menu bar icon\nto make a sticker" }
        return "Press \(shortcut.label)\nto make a sticker"
    }

    /// The three masters in `LibraryStore.tutorialIDs` order; nil when one
    /// can't be drawn (Maple's asset missing).
    static func masters(shortcut: Shortcut?) -> [CGImage]? {
        guard let first = shortcutCard(for: shortcut),
              let second = card(text: doubleClickText, fill: mint, textColor: ink),
              let maple = BundledImages.cgImage(named: BundledImages.mapleName),
              let third = mapleWithBubble(maple)
        else { return nil }
        return [first, second, third]
    }

    static func shortcutCard(for shortcut: Shortcut?) -> CGImage? {
        card(text: shortcutText(for: shortcut), fill: blue, textColor: .white)
    }

    static func roundedFont(size: CGFloat, weight: NSFont.Weight) -> NSFont {
        let base = NSFont.systemFont(ofSize: size, weight: weight)
        guard let descriptor = base.fontDescriptor.withDesign(.rounded),
              let rounded = NSFont(descriptor: descriptor, size: size)
        else { return base }
        return rounded
    }

    /// A text card: each line centred, on `fill`, inside a white border.
    static func card(text: String, fill: NSColor, textColor: NSColor) -> CGImage? {
        let font = roundedFont(size: cardFontSize, weight: .semibold)
        let attributes: [NSAttributedString.Key: Any] = [.font: font, .foregroundColor: textColor]
        let lines = text.components(separatedBy: "\n").map { NSAttributedString(string: $0, attributes: attributes) }
        let lineHeight = ceil(font.ascender - font.descender + font.leading)
        let textWidth = ceil(lines.map { $0.size().width }.max() ?? 0)
        let textHeight = lineHeight * CGFloat(lines.count) + lineGap * CGFloat(max(lines.count - 1, 0))
        let outer = CGRect(x: edge, y: edge, width: textWidth + 2 * (cardPadding.width + cardBorder),
                           height: textHeight + 2 * (cardPadding.height + cardBorder))
        return draw(size: CGSize(width: outer.width + 2 * edge, height: outer.height + 2 * edge)) { context in
            fillRoundedRect(outer, radius: min(cardRadius + cardBorder, outer.height / 2), color: .white, in: context)
            let inner = outer.insetBy(dx: cardBorder, dy: cardBorder)
            fillRoundedRect(inner, radius: min(cardRadius, inner.height / 2), color: fill, in: context)
            var top = inner.maxY - cardPadding.height
            for line in lines {
                let size = line.size()
                line.draw(at: CGPoint(x: inner.midX - size.width / 2, y: top - lineHeight + (lineHeight - size.height) / 2))
                top -= lineHeight + lineGap
            }
        }
    }

    /// Maple (her asset is 2 px per point) with the pink bubble on her left.
    static func mapleWithBubble(_ maple: CGImage) -> CGImage? {
        let mapleSize = CGSize(width: CGFloat(maple.width) / pixelsPerPoint, height: CGFloat(maple.height) / pixelsPerPoint)
        let bubbleLeft = bubbleBorder
        let mapleLeft = bubbleLeft + bubbleSize.width - bubbleOverlap
        let height = max(mapleSize.height, bubbleTop + 62 + bubbleBorder)
        let size = CGSize(width: mapleLeft + mapleSize.width, height: height)
        return draw(size: size) { context in
            context.interpolationQuality = .high
            context.draw(maple, in: CGRect(x: mapleLeft, y: height - mapleSize.height,
                                           width: mapleSize.width, height: mapleSize.height))
            // The website's SVG path, in its own top-down coordinates.
            var flip = CGAffineTransform(translationX: bubbleLeft, y: height - bubbleTop).scaledBy(x: 1, y: -1)
            guard let path = bubblePath().copy(using: &flip) else { return }
            context.addPath(path)
            context.setLineJoin(.round)
            context.setLineWidth(bubbleBorder * 2)
            context.setStrokeColor(NSColor.white.cgColor)
            context.setFillColor(NSColor.white.cgColor)
            context.drawPath(using: .fillStroke)
            context.addPath(path)
            context.setFillColor(pink.cgColor)
            context.fillPath()
            let font = roundedFont(size: bubbleFontSize, weight: .medium)
            let text = NSAttributedString(string: bubbleText, attributes: [.font: font, .foregroundColor: NSColor.white])
            let textSize = text.size()
            let body = CGRect(x: bubbleLeft, y: height - bubbleTop - bubbleSize.height,
                              width: bubbleSize.width, height: bubbleSize.height)
            text.draw(at: CGPoint(x: body.midX - textSize.width / 2, y: body.midY - textSize.height / 2))
        }
    }

    /// `M 18 0 H 304 C 314 0 322 8 322 18 V 26 C 322 32 317 36 310 39 C 313 46 316 52 322 58
    /// C 323 60 321 62 319 61 C 307 57 299 51 294 44 H 18 C 8 44 0 36 0 26 V 18 C 0 8 8 0 18 0 Z`
    static func bubblePath() -> CGPath {
        let path = CGMutablePath()
        path.move(to: CGPoint(x: 18, y: 0))
        path.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: 304, y: 0))
        path.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: 322, y: 18), control1: CGPoint(x: 314, y: 0), control2: CGPoint(x: 322, y: 8))
        path.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: 322, y: 26))
        path.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: 310, y: 39), control1: CGPoint(x: 322, y: 32), control2: CGPoint(x: 317, y: 36))
        path.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: 322, y: 58), control1: CGPoint(x: 313, y: 46), control2: CGPoint(x: 316, y: 52))
        path.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: 319, y: 61), control1: CGPoint(x: 323, y: 60), control2: CGPoint(x: 321, y: 62))
        path.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: 294, y: 44), control1: CGPoint(x: 307, y: 57), control2: CGPoint(x: 299, y: 51))
        path.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: 18, y: 44))
        path.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: 0, y: 26), control1: CGPoint(x: 8, y: 44), control2: CGPoint(x: 0, y: 36))
        path.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: 0, y: 18))
        path.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: 18, y: 0), control1: CGPoint(x: 0, y: 8), control2: CGPoint(x: 8, y: 0))
        path.closeSubpath()
        return path
    }

    private static func fillRoundedRect(_ rect: CGRect, radius: CGFloat, color: NSColor, in context: CGContext) {
        context.addPath(CGPath(roundedRect: rect, cornerWidth: radius, cornerHeight: radius, transform: nil))
        context.setFillColor(color.cgColor)
        context.fillPath()
    }

    /// An sRGB, premultiplied bitmap of `size` points at `pixelsPerPoint`,
    /// y up, with AppKit text drawing pointed at it.
    private static func draw(size: CGSize, _ body: (CGContext) -> Void) -> CGImage? {
        let width = Int((size.width * pixelsPerPoint).rounded(.up))
        let height = Int((size.height * pixelsPerPoint).rounded(.up))
        guard width > 0, height > 0, let space = CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB),
              let context = CGContext(data: nil, width: width, height: height, bitsPerComponent: 8, bytesPerRow: 0,
                                      space: space, bitmapInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo.premultipliedLast.rawValue)
        else { return nil }
        context.scaleBy(x: pixelsPerPoint, y: pixelsPerPoint)
        NSGraphicsContext.saveGraphicsState()
        NSGraphicsContext.current = NSGraphicsContext(cgContext: context, flipped: false)
        body(context)
        NSGraphicsContext.restoreGraphicsState()
        return context.makeImage()
    }
}

/// The first-run tutorial stickers (spec section 13). On a user launch with
/// `didFirstRun` false and no stickers in the library, the three masters are
/// drawn once and saved as ordinary `stickers/<uuid>.png` entries under the
/// fixed `LibraryStore.tutorialIDs` with `tutorial: true` (PNGs first, then
/// the index), placed on the main display in Shadow, and `didFirstRun` is
/// set once the index is saved. They are never made again: removing one
/// deletes it outright (`StickerManager.remove`), and they never show in the
/// history or count towards the support ask. While tutorial 1 exists, a
/// shortcut change redraws its master in place and re-renders its placements.
@MainActor
final class TutorialStickers {
    static let style: StickerStyle = .shadow

    private let library: LibraryController
    private let stickers: StickerManager
    private let screens: ScreenProvider
    private let defaults: UserDefaults
    private let log = Logger(subsystem: "com.ethan.stickermine", category: "tutorial")

    init(library: LibraryController, stickers: StickerManager, screens: ScreenProvider, defaults: UserDefaults) {
        self.library = library
        self.stickers = stickers
        self.screens = screens
        self.defaults = defaults
    }

    /// Draws, saves and places the three tutorials, clear of a Home window of
    /// `homeSize` centred on the main display. `completion` says whether the
    /// index was saved (and `didFirstRun` set).
    func placeFirstRun(shortcut: Shortcut?, homeSize: CGSize, completion: ((Bool) -> Void)? = nil) {
        guard !library.isReadOnly, let display = screens.mainDisplay,
              let images = TutorialArt.masters(shortcut: shortcut)
        else {
            log.error("first run: no main display or no artwork")
            completion?(false)
            return
        }
        let pngs = zip(LibraryStore.tutorialIDs, images).compactMap { id, image in
            (try? MasterImage.pngData(image, pixelsPerPoint: Double(TutorialArt.pixelsPerPoint))).map { (id, $0) }
        }
        guard pngs.count == images.count else {
            completion?(false)
            return
        }
        let store = library.store
        Task { [weak self] in
            let written = await Self.write(pngs, to: store)
            self?.firstRunWritten(images, written: written, display: display, homeSize: homeSize,
                                  completion: completion)
        }
    }

    /// Writes PNGs off the main actor (spec section 12).
    nonisolated private static func write(_ pngs: [(UUID, Data)], to store: LibraryStore) async -> Bool {
        do {
            for (id, data) in pngs { try store.writePNG(data, for: id) }
            return true
        } catch {
            return false
        }
    }

    private func firstRunWritten(_ images: [CGImage], written: Bool, display: DisplayInfo, homeSize: CGSize,
                                 completion: ((Bool) -> Void)?) {
        let ids = LibraryStore.tutorialIDs
        guard written, !library.isReadOnly else {
            for id in ids { try? library.store.deletePNG(for: id) }
            log.error("first run: writing the tutorial PNGs failed")
            completion?(false)
            return
        }
        let created = Date()
        let records = zip(ids, images).map { id, image in
            StickerRecord(id: id, created: created, pixelWidth: image.width, pixelHeight: image.height,
                          pixelsPerPoint: Double(TutorialArt.pixelsPerPoint), lastStyle: Self.style, tutorial: true)
        }
        let items = TutorialLayout.layout(naturalSizes: records.map(\.naturalPointSize), frame: display.frame,
                                          visibleFrame: display.visibleFrame, homeSize: homeSize)
        // First-run tutorial placement is one of the spec's write exceptions (section 11).
        let key = stickers.recordKeyForUserEdit(on: display)
        library.mutate { library in
            library.stickers.removeAll { ids.contains($0.id) }
            library.stickers.append(contentsOf: records)
            for (index, record) in records.enumerated() {
                let placement = Placement(sticker: record.id, style: Self.style,
                                          rotation: TutorialLayout.rotations[index],
                                          poses: [display.size.key: items[index].pose])
                library.displays[key]?.placements.append(placement)
            }
        }
        library.flush()
        let saved = !library.hasUnsavedChanges
        if saved { defaults.didFirstRun = true }
        stickers.reconcileNow()
        log.notice("first run: tutorial stickers placed (saved: \(saved))")
        completion?(saved)
    }

    /// The capture shortcut changed: while tutorial 1 exists, its master is
    /// redrawn with the new shortcut (PNG first, then the index's pixel size)
    /// and its placements re-rendered.
    func shortcutChanged(to shortcut: Shortcut?, completion: (() -> Void)? = nil) {
        let id = LibraryStore.tutorialIDs[0]
        guard !library.isReadOnly, library.library.sticker(id) != nil,
              let image = TutorialArt.shortcutCard(for: shortcut),
              let data = try? MasterImage.pngData(image, pixelsPerPoint: Double(TutorialArt.pixelsPerPoint))
        else {
            completion?()
            return
        }
        let store = library.store
        Task { [weak self] in
            let written = await Self.write([(id, data)], to: store)
            self?.shortcutCardWritten(id, image: image, written: written)
            completion?()
        }
    }

    private func shortcutCardWritten(_ id: UUID, image: CGImage, written: Bool) {
        guard written, !library.isReadOnly else { return }
        guard library.library.sticker(id) != nil else {
            // Removed while the PNG was written: launch repair would adopt it.
            try? library.store.deletePNG(for: id)
            return
        }
        library.mutate { library in
            guard let index = library.stickers.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
            library.stickers[index].pixelWidth = image.width
            library.stickers[index].pixelHeight = image.height
        }
        stickers.masterChanged(for: id)
    }
}
```

### Task 10: SupportAsk: the one-time card

**Files:**
- Create: `Stickermine/Onboarding/SupportAsk.swift`
- Test: `StickermineTests/SupportAskTests.swift` (create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `UserDefaults.ownPlacementsCount` and `supportAskShown` (Task 1); `PanelPolicy.applyCommon(to:hidesOnDeactivate:)` (M1); `FirstClickHostingView` (M5, `Stickers/ToolbarPill.swift`: controls take the first click in a window that isn't key).
- Produces:

```text
enum SupportAskRule {                                                     // pure
    static let threshold: Int                                             // 5
    static func afterPlacement(count: Int, alreadyShown: Bool, isTutorial: Bool) -> (count: Int, show: Bool)
}
@MainActor final class SupportAskPanel: NSPanel                           // canBecomeKey true (once clicked), canBecomeMain false
@MainActor final class SupportAsk {
    nonisolated static let message, coffeeTitle, laterTitle: String; nonisolated static let kofiURL: URL
    nonisolated static let showDelay: TimeInterval                        // 0.5
    nonisolated static let gap, edgeInset, shadowPadding: CGFloat         // 4, 8, 18
    init(defaults: UserDefaults = .standard, openURL: @escaping (URL) -> Void = { NSWorkspace.shared.open($0) },
         anchor: @escaping () -> CGRect?, fallbackArea: @escaping () -> CGRect?, showDelay: TimeInterval = SupportAsk.showDelay)
    private(set) var panel: SupportAskPanel?; var isVisible: Bool { get }
    func placementClicked(isTutorial: Bool)                               // SessionCoordinator.onPlaced (Task 14)
    func show(); func dismiss()                                           // dismiss: declined for good
    static func makePanel() -> SupportAskPanel
    nonisolated static func frame(size: CGSize, anchor: CGRect?, visibleFrame: CGRect) -> CGRect   // pure
}
```

How each dismissal is caught: the two buttons call `dismiss()` (Buy me a coffee opens Ko-fi first); Esc works through Maybe later's `.keyboardShortcut(.cancelAction)` once the user has clicked into the card (the panel can become key then, without activating Stickermine; it is never made key when it appears); a click anywhere else is seen by a global mouse-down monitor (other apps; mouse monitors need no permission, unlike key monitors) and a local one (Stickermine's own windows, stickers included); quitting runs `dismiss()` in the termination cleanup (Task 14). `supportAskShown` is set in `show()`, so every one of these, and a quit, counts as declined.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the support ask**

`Stickermine/Onboarding/SupportAsk.swift` (complete file):

```swift
import AppKit
import SwiftUI

/// When the one-time support ask appears (spec section 13). Pure.
enum SupportAskRule {
    static let threshold = 5

    /// After a placement click: the new `ownPlacementsCount` and whether the
    /// ask shows now. Tutorial stickers don't count; nothing decrements it.
    static func afterPlacement(count: Int, alreadyShown: Bool, isTutorial: Bool) -> (count: Int, show: Bool) {
        guard !isTutorial else { return (count, false) }
        let next = count + 1
        return (next, !alreadyShown && next >= threshold)
    }
}

/// The card's panel. Never made key when it appears (the frontmost app keeps
/// focus); a click into it makes it key without activating Stickermine, so
/// Esc works then.
@MainActor
final class SupportAskPanel: NSPanel {
    override var canBecomeKey: Bool { true }
    override var canBecomeMain: Bool { false }
}

/// The one-time support ask (spec section 13): the first time
/// `ownPlacementsCount` reaches 5 and `supportAskShown` is false, a small
/// sticker-styled card appears below the menu bar icon (top-right of the main
/// display while the icon is hidden), without activating Stickermine or
/// taking focus from the frontmost app. `supportAskShown` is set when it
/// appears; every way it goes (either button, Esc, a click anywhere else,
/// quitting) counts as declined, and it never appears again.
@MainActor
final class SupportAsk {
    nonisolated static let message = "Enjoying Stickermine? It’s free and open source. If it made you smile, you can buy me a coffee."
    nonisolated static let coffeeTitle = "☕️ Buy me a coffee"
    nonisolated static let laterTitle = "Maybe later"
    nonisolated static let kofiURL = URL(string: "https://ko-fi.com/grokcodile")!
    /// After the placement click, so its own mouse-up is over.
    nonisolated static let showDelay: TimeInterval = 0.5
    nonisolated static let gap: CGFloat = 4
    nonisolated static let edgeInset: CGFloat = 8
    /// Transparent room around the card for its shadow; the frame lets it
    /// overhang, so the card itself lines up with the icon.
    nonisolated static let shadowPadding: CGFloat = 18

    private let defaults: UserDefaults
    private let openURL: (URL) -> Void
    private let anchor: () -> CGRect?
    private let fallbackArea: () -> CGRect?
    private let showDelay: TimeInterval
    /// The card's panel while it is up.
    private(set) var panel: SupportAskPanel?
    private var monitors: [Any] = []
    private var showPending = false

    /// `anchor` is the menu bar icon's frame on screen (nil while hidden);
    /// `fallbackArea` the main display's visible frame.
    init(defaults: UserDefaults = .standard, openURL: @escaping (URL) -> Void = { NSWorkspace.shared.open($0) },
         anchor: @escaping () -> CGRect?, fallbackArea: @escaping () -> CGRect?,
         showDelay: TimeInterval = SupportAsk.showDelay) {
        self.defaults = defaults
        self.openURL = openURL
        self.anchor = anchor
        self.fallbackArea = fallbackArea
        self.showDelay = showDelay
    }

    var isVisible: Bool { panel?.isVisible == true }

    /// A placement click (`SessionCoordinator.onPlaced`); cross-display drags
    /// never get here.
    func placementClicked(isTutorial: Bool) {
        let result = SupportAskRule.afterPlacement(count: defaults.ownPlacementsCount,
                                                   alreadyShown: defaults.supportAskShown, isTutorial: isTutorial)
        guard !isTutorial else { return }
        defaults.ownPlacementsCount = result.count
        guard result.show, !showPending, panel == nil else { return }
        if showDelay > 0 {
            showPending = true
            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + showDelay) { [weak self] in
                MainActor.assumeIsolated {
                    self?.showPending = false
                    self?.show()
                }
            }
        } else {
            show()
        }
    }

    /// Shows the card (once) and marks the ask as shown.
    func show() {
        guard panel == nil else { return }
        defaults.supportAskShown = true
        let panel = Self.makePanel()
        let host = FirstClickHostingView(rootView: SupportAskView(
            coffee: { [weak self] in self?.buyCoffee() },
            later: { [weak self] in self?.dismiss() }))
        panel.contentView = host
        let anchorRect = anchor()
        let area = anchorRect.flatMap(Self.visibleFrame(containing:)) ?? fallbackArea() ?? .zero
        panel.setFrame(Self.frame(size: host.fittingSize, anchor: anchorRect, visibleFrame: area), display: false)
        panel.orderFrontRegardless()
        self.panel = panel
        installMonitors(for: panel)
    }

    /// Declines: the card goes and never comes back.
    func dismiss() {
        monitors.forEach(NSEvent.removeMonitor)
        monitors = []
        guard let panel else { return }
        self.panel = nil
        panel.orderOut(nil)
        panel.close()
    }

    private func buyCoffee() {
        openURL(Self.kofiURL)
        dismiss()
    }

    /// The panel policy (spec section 4): non-activating, never hidden on
    /// deactivation, transparent around the card, the level last.
    static func makePanel() -> SupportAskPanel {
        let panel = SupportAskPanel(contentRect: NSRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 332, height: 150),
                                    styleMask: [.borderless, .nonactivatingPanel], backing: .buffered, defer: false)
        PanelPolicy.applyCommon(to: panel, hidesOnDeactivate: false)
        panel.isOpaque = false
        panel.backgroundColor = .clear
        panel.hasShadow = false
        panel.animationBehavior = .none
        panel.collectionBehavior = [.canJoinAllSpaces, .fullScreenAuxiliary, .ignoresCycle]
        panel.level = .statusBar  // last
        return panel
    }

    /// The panel's frame for a card view of `size` (its shadow padding
    /// included): the card's top-right corner just below the icon's
    /// bottom-right corner; without the icon, `edgeInset` from the top-right
    /// corner of `visibleFrame`. The card always stays inside `visibleFrame`.
    nonisolated static func frame(size: CGSize, anchor: CGRect?, visibleFrame: CGRect) -> CGRect {
        let card = CGSize(width: size.width - 2 * shadowPadding, height: size.height - 2 * shadowPadding)
        var right: CGFloat
        var top: CGFloat
        if let anchor {
            right = anchor.maxX
            top = anchor.minY - gap
        } else {
            right = visibleFrame.maxX - edgeInset
            top = visibleFrame.maxY - edgeInset
        }
        right = max(min(right, visibleFrame.maxX - edgeInset), visibleFrame.minX + edgeInset + card.width)
        top = max(min(top, visibleFrame.maxY), visibleFrame.minY + edgeInset + card.height)
        return CGRect(x: (right - card.width - shadowPadding).rounded(), y: (top - card.height - shadowPadding).rounded(),
                      width: size.width, height: size.height)
    }

    /// The visible frame of the screen the icon is on.
    private static func visibleFrame(containing rect: CGRect) -> CGRect? {
        NSScreen.screens.first { $0.frame.intersects(rect) }?.visibleFrame
    }

    /// A click anywhere else declines: in other apps (global monitor, no
    /// permission needed for mouse events) and in Stickermine's own windows
    /// (local monitor).
    private func installMonitors(for panel: NSPanel) {
        let mask: NSEvent.EventTypeMask = [.leftMouseDown, .rightMouseDown, .otherMouseDown]
        if let global = NSEvent.addGlobalMonitorForEvents(matching: mask, handler: { [weak self] _ in
            MainActor.assumeIsolated { self?.dismiss() }
        }) {
            monitors.append(global)
        }
        if let local = NSEvent.addLocalMonitorForEvents(matching: mask, handler: { [weak self, weak panel] event in
            if event.window !== panel {
                MainActor.assumeIsolated { self?.dismiss() }
            }
            return event
        }) {
            monitors.append(local)
        }
    }
}

/// The card: yellow (the website's coffee colour) with a white border.
private struct SupportAskView: View {
    let coffee: () -> Void
    let later: () -> Void

    private static let yellow = Color(red: 0xFF / 255.0, green: 0xD8 / 255.0, blue: 0x4D / 255.0)
    private static let ink = Color(red: 0x1F / 255.0, green: 0x23 / 255.0, blue: 0x40 / 255.0)

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            Text(SupportAsk.message)
                .font(.system(size: 14, weight: .medium, design: .rounded))
                .foregroundStyle(Self.ink)
                .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
            HStack(spacing: 14) {
                Button(action: coffee) {
                    Text(SupportAsk.coffeeTitle)
                        .font(.system(size: 13, weight: .semibold, design: .rounded))
                        .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 14)
                        .padding(.vertical, 7)
                        .background(Capsule().fill(Self.ink))
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                Button(action: later) {
                    Text(SupportAsk.laterTitle)
                        .font(.system(size: 13, weight: .medium, design: .rounded))
                        .foregroundStyle(Self.ink.opacity(0.75))
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .keyboardShortcut(.cancelAction)
            }
        }
        .padding(18)
        .frame(width: 280, alignment: .leading)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 24, style: .continuous).fill(Self.yellow))
        .padding(8)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 32, style: .continuous).fill(Color.white))
        .shadow(color: Self.ink.opacity(0.22), radius: 12, x: 0, y: 6)
        .padding(SupportAsk.shadowPadding)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the history rules**

`Stickermine/Home/HistoryGrid.swift` (complete file):

```swift
import AppKit
import ImageIO
import SwiftUI

/// Which stickers the history grid shows, and what deleting one says (spec
/// sections 12 and 13). Pure.
enum HistoryList {
    /// Every non-tutorial sticker, newest first (ties by ID, so the order is stable).
    static func items(in library: Library) -> [StickerRecord] {
        library.stickers
            .filter { !$0.tutorial }
            .sorted { ($0.created, $0.id.uuidString) > ($1.created, $1.id.uuidString) }
    }

    static let deleteTitle = "Delete from History?"

    /// The confirmation states how many placements go with it (on every
    /// display, connected or not).
    static func deleteMessage(placementCount: Int) -> String {
        switch placementCount {
        case 0: return "This sticker isn’t on your desktop. Deleting it can’t be undone."
        case 1: return "This also removes it from 1 place on your desktops. Deleting it can’t be undone."
        default: return "This also removes it from \(placementCount) places on your desktops. Deleting it can’t be undone."
        }
    }
}

/// The session as Delete from History sees it (`SessionCoordinator`; tests use a fake).
@MainActor
protocol StickerSession: AnyObject {
    /// The sticker the running session works on, once known.
    var currentSticker: UUID? { get }
    func cancel()
}

extension SessionCoordinator: StickerSession {}

/// Delete from History (spec section 12).
@MainActor
enum HistoryDeletion {
    /// Cancels a session working on the sticker (a re-place from history, or
    /// a fresh one already saved), then deletes it everywhere: editing of any
    /// of its placements ends (a gesture is discarded), every placement on
    /// every display goes, then the PNG, then the entry (`StickerManager`).
    /// A render that finishes later finds its placement gone and is dropped.
    static func delete(_ sticker: UUID, session: StickerSession?, stickers: StickerManager,
                       thumbnails: ThumbnailCache?) {
        if let session, session.currentSticker == sticker { session.cancel() }
        stickers.deleteSticker(sticker)
        thumbnails?.remove(sticker)
    }
}

/// History thumbnails: `CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex` at 256 px at
/// most, kept in memory (spec section 12). Loaded off the main thread.
@MainActor
final class ThumbnailCache {
    nonisolated static let maxPixelSize = 256

    private let cache = NSCache<NSUUID, CGImage>()

    init() {}

    func cached(_ sticker: UUID) -> CGImage? {
        cache.object(forKey: sticker as NSUUID)
    }

    func thumbnail(for sticker: UUID, at url: URL) async -> CGImage? {
        if let hit = cached(sticker) { return hit }
        let image = await Task.detached(priority: .utility) { Self.makeThumbnail(at: url) }.value
        if let image { cache.setObject(image, forKey: sticker as NSUUID) }
        return image
    }

    func remove(_ sticker: UUID) {
        cache.removeObject(forKey: sticker as NSUUID)
    }

    /// The PNG scaled so its long side is at most `maxPixelSize`.
    nonisolated static func makeThumbnail(at url: URL, maxPixelSize: Int = ThumbnailCache.maxPixelSize) -> CGImage? {
        guard let source = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL(url as CFURL, nil) else { return nil }
        let options: [CFString: Any] = [
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
            kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: maxPixelSize,
        ]
        return CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(source, 0, options as CFDictionary)
    }
}
```

**Files:**

- Test: `StickermineTests/HomeModelTests.swift` (create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LibraryController` (`library`, `isReadOnly`, `store`, M2); `Library.placementCount(of:)` (M2); `ScreenRecordingAccess` (M4); `Settings` accessors (Task 1); `SystemShortcuts.conflicts(with:)` (Task 2); `LoginItem` (Task 5); `Updater` (Task 6); `HistoryList`, `ThumbnailCache` (Task 11); `LoginItemTests.FakeService` (test support from Task 5).
- Produces:

```text
@MainActor final class HomeModel: ObservableObject {
    struct Actions {                                                      // every member defaults to doing nothing
        var newSticker: () -> Void; var replace: (UUID) -> Void; var delete: (UUID) -> Void
        var shortcutChanged: (Shortcut?) -> Void; var recordingChanged: (Bool) -> Void
        var menuBarIconChanged: (Bool) -> Void; var sharingHiddenChanged: (Bool) -> Void
        var fixScreenRecording: () -> Void; var relaunch: () -> Void; var quit: () -> Void
    }
    struct PendingDelete: Equatable, Identifiable { let id: UUID; let message: String }
    static let emptyHistoryText: String
    @Published private(set) var history: [StickerRecord]; @Published private(set) var isReadOnly: Bool
    @Published var sessionActive: Bool
    @Published private(set) var shortcut: Shortcut?; shortcutConflicts: Bool; chordRejected: Bool
    @Published private(set) var showMenuBarIcon: Bool; loginState: LoginItem.State; hideFromSharing: Bool
    @Published private(set) var screenRecordingOn: Bool; screenRecordingRequested: Bool
    @Published var pendingDelete: PendingDelete?
    let library: LibraryController; let thumbnails: ThumbnailCache; let updater: Updater
    init(library: LibraryController, defaults: UserDefaults, loginItem: LoginItem, access: ScreenRecordingAccess,
         thumbnails: ThumbnailCache, updater: Updater,
         conflicts: @escaping (Shortcut) -> Bool = { SystemShortcuts.conflicts(with: $0) }, actions: Actions)
    func refresh(); func libraryChanged(); func pngURL(for sticker: UUID) -> URL
    func newSticker(); func replace(_ sticker: UUID)                      // ignored while read-only or in a session
    func requestDelete(_ sticker: UUID); func confirmDelete(); func cancelDelete()
    func setShortcut(_ shortcut: Shortcut?); func recordingChanged(_ recording: Bool); func rejectChord()
    func setShowMenuBarIcon(_ on: Bool); func setLaunchAtLogin(_ on: Bool); func openLoginItems()
    func setHideFromSharing(_ on: Bool); func fixScreenRecording(); func relaunch(); func quit()
}
```

The model writes each setting to `UserDefaults` itself and then tells the app (`Actions`) to apply it live: a new shortcut is stored before `shortcutChanged` runs, so the hotkey, the menu and tutorial 1 all read the same value.

```swift
import AppKit
import Carbon.HIToolbox
import ServiceManagement
import Testing
@testable import Stickermine

/// Home's model without a window: a temporary library, its own defaults
/// suite, a fake login item service, a fake Screen Recording check and
/// recorded actions.
@MainActor
struct HomeModelTests {
    final class Recorded {
        var newStickers = 0
        var replaced: [UUID] = []
        var deleted: [UUID] = []
        var shortcuts: [Shortcut?] = []
        var recording: [Bool] = []
        var menuBarIcon: [Bool] = []
        var sharingHidden: [Bool] = []
        var permissionPanels = 0
        var relaunches = 0
        var quits = 0
    }

    final class FakeAccess: ScreenRecordingAccess {
        var granted = false
        func preflight() -> Bool { granted }
        func request() -> Bool { granted }
    }

    struct Harness {
        let model: HomeModel
        let library: LibraryController
        let defaults: UserDefaults
        let suiteName: String
        let folder: URL
        let recorded: Recorded
        let access: FakeAccess
        let service: LoginItemTests.FakeService

        func finish() {
            defaults.removePersistentDomain(forName: suiteName)
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: folder)
        }
    }

    static func harness(_ stickers: [StickerRecord] = [], placements: [Placement] = [], readOnly: Bool = false,
                        conflicts: @escaping (Shortcut) -> Bool = { _ in false }) throws -> Harness {
        let folder = Fixtures.temporaryDirectory()
        let store = LibraryStore(root: folder)
        if readOnly {
            try Data(#"{"version": 99, "stickers": [], "displays": {}}"#.utf8)
                .write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent("library.json"))
        } else {
            for sticker in stickers {
                try store.writePNG(Fixtures.pngData(width: 40, height: 40, pixelsPerPoint: 2), for: sticker.id)
            }
            let displays = placements.isEmpty ? [:]
                : [Fixtures.externalUUID: Fixtures.displayRecord(for: Fixtures.external(), placements: placements)]
            try store.save(Library(stickers: stickers, displays: displays))
        }
        let library = LibraryController(store: store, saveDelay: 60)
        let suiteName = "StickermineTests-\(UUID().uuidString)"
        let defaults = UserDefaults(suiteName: suiteName)!
        Settings.register(in: defaults)
        let recorded = Recorded()
        let access = FakeAccess()
        let service = LoginItemTests.FakeService(status: .enabled)
        var actions = HomeModel.Actions()
        actions.newSticker = { recorded.newStickers += 1 }
        actions.replace = { recorded.replaced.append($0) }
        actions.delete = { recorded.deleted.append($0) }
        actions.shortcutChanged = { recorded.shortcuts.append($0) }
        actions.recordingChanged = { recorded.recording.append($0) }
        actions.menuBarIconChanged = { recorded.menuBarIcon.append($0) }
        actions.sharingHiddenChanged = { recorded.sharingHidden.append($0) }
        actions.fixScreenRecording = { recorded.permissionPanels += 1 }
        actions.relaunch = { recorded.relaunches += 1 }
        actions.quit = { recorded.quits += 1 }
        let model = HomeModel(library: library, defaults: defaults,
                              loginItem: LoginItem(service: service, defaults: defaults), access: access,
                              thumbnails: ThumbnailCache(),
                              updater: Updater(appVersion: "1.0", fetch: { nil }, beforeHelper: {}),
                              conflicts: conflicts, actions: actions)
        return Harness(model: model, library: library, defaults: defaults, suiteName: suiteName, folder: folder,
                       recorded: recorded, access: access, service: service)
    }

    private func record(_ id: UUID = UUID(), at seconds: TimeInterval, tutorial: Bool = false) -> StickerRecord {
        StickerRecord(id: id, created: Date(timeIntervalSince1970: seconds), pixelWidth: 40, pixelHeight: 40,
                      pixelsPerPoint: 2, lastStyle: .outline, tutorial: tutorial)
    }

    @Test func theHistoryIsEveryOwnStickerNewestFirstAndFollowsTheLibrary() throws {
        let old = record(at: 100), new = record(at: 200)
        let tutorial = record(LibraryStore.tutorialIDs[2], at: 300, tutorial: true)
        let h = try Self.harness([old, tutorial, new])
        defer { h.finish() }
        #expect(h.model.history.map(\.id) == [new.id, old.id])
        let newest = record(at: 400)
        h.library.mutate { $0.stickers.append(newest) }
        h.model.libraryChanged()
        #expect(h.model.history.map(\.id) == [newest.id, new.id, old.id])
        #expect(h.model.pngURL(for: old.id) == h.library.store.pngURL(for: old.id))
    }

    @Test func newStickerAndReplaceAreIgnoredDuringASession() throws {
        let sticker = record(at: 100)
        let h = try Self.harness([sticker])
        defer { h.finish() }
        h.model.newSticker()
        h.model.replace(sticker.id)
        #expect(h.recorded.newStickers == 1)
        #expect(h.recorded.replaced == [sticker.id])
        h.model.sessionActive = true
        h.model.newSticker()
        h.model.replace(sticker.id)
        #expect(h.recorded.newStickers == 1)
        #expect(h.recorded.replaced == [sticker.id])
    }

    @Test func deleteAsksWithThePlacementCountThenDeletes() throws {
        let sticker = record(at: 100)
        let placements = [EditingFixtures.placement(sticker.id), EditingFixtures.placement(sticker.id)]
        let h = try Self.harness([sticker], placements: placements)
        defer { h.finish() }
        h.model.requestDelete(sticker.id)
        #expect(h.model.pendingDelete == HomeModel.PendingDelete(id: sticker.id,
                                                                 message: HistoryList.deleteMessage(placementCount: 2)))
        h.model.cancelDelete()
        #expect(h.model.pendingDelete == nil)
        #expect(h.recorded.deleted.isEmpty)
        h.model.requestDelete(sticker.id)
        h.model.confirmDelete()
        #expect(h.recorded.deleted == [sticker.id])
        #expect(h.model.pendingDelete == nil)
        h.model.requestDelete(UUID())
        #expect(h.model.pendingDelete == nil)
    }

    @Test func aReadOnlyLibraryShowsTheNoticeAndChangesNothing() throws {
        let h = try Self.harness(readOnly: true)
        defer { h.finish() }
        #expect(h.model.isReadOnly)
        h.model.newSticker()
        h.model.replace(UUID())
        #expect(h.recorded.newStickers == 0)
        #expect(h.recorded.replaced.isEmpty)
        #expect(HUD.readOnlyMessage == "This library was saved by a newer Stickermine. Update to make changes.")
    }

    @Test func aRecordedShortcutIsStoredCheckedAndPassedOn() throws {
        let clash = Shortcut(keyCode: 20, modifiers: UInt32(cmdKey | shiftKey))
        let h = try Self.harness(conflicts: { $0 == clash })
        defer { h.finish() }
        #expect(h.model.shortcut == .defaultCapture)
        #expect(!h.model.shortcutConflicts)
        h.model.recordingChanged(true)
        h.model.setShortcut(clash)
        h.model.recordingChanged(false)
        #expect(h.defaults.captureShortcut == clash)
        #expect(h.model.shortcutConflicts)
        h.model.setShortcut(nil)
        #expect(h.defaults.captureShortcut == nil)
        #expect(!h.model.shortcutConflicts)
        #expect(h.recorded.shortcuts == [clash, nil])
        #expect(h.recorded.recording == [true, false])
    }

    @Test func aRefusedChordShowsUntilTheNextRecording() throws {
        let h = try Self.harness()
        defer { h.finish() }
        h.model.rejectChord()
        #expect(h.model.chordRejected)
        h.model.recordingChanged(true)
        #expect(!h.model.chordRejected)
        h.model.rejectChord()
        h.model.setShortcut(.defaultCapture)
        #expect(!h.model.chordRejected)
    }

    @Test func theTogglesWriteTheirSettingsAndActLive() throws {
        let h = try Self.harness()
        defer { h.finish() }
        h.model.setShowMenuBarIcon(false)
        h.model.setHideFromSharing(false)
        #expect(!h.defaults.showMenuBarIcon)
        #expect(!h.defaults.hideFromSharing)
        #expect(!h.model.showMenuBarIcon)
        #expect(!h.model.hideFromSharing)
        #expect(h.recorded.menuBarIcon == [false])
        #expect(h.recorded.sharingHidden == [false])
        h.model.setLaunchAtLogin(false)
        #expect(h.model.loginState == .off)
        #expect(h.service.unregisters == 1)
        h.service.statusAfterRegister = .requiresApproval
        h.model.setLaunchAtLogin(true)
        #expect(h.model.loginState == .requiresApproval)
        #expect(h.service.settingsOpened == 1)
        h.model.openLoginItems()
        #expect(h.service.settingsOpened == 2)
        h.model.quit()
        #expect(h.recorded.quits == 1)
    }

    @Test func screenRecordingIsReadAgainOnRefresh() throws {
        let h = try Self.harness()
        defer { h.finish() }
        #expect(!h.model.screenRecordingOn)
        h.model.fixScreenRecording()
        #expect(h.recorded.permissionPanels == 1)
        #expect(!h.model.screenRecordingRequested)
        h.defaults.didRequestScreenCapture = true
        h.model.refresh()
        #expect(h.model.screenRecordingRequested)
        h.model.relaunch()
        #expect(h.recorded.relaunches == 1)
        h.access.granted = true
        #expect(!h.model.screenRecordingOn)
        h.model.refresh()
        #expect(h.model.screenRecordingOn)
    }
}
```

```swift
import AppKit
import SwiftUI

/// What Home shows and does (spec section 13): the history, the settings,
/// the read-only notice and the session state. Writes the settings to
/// `UserDefaults` and asks the rest of the app to act through `Actions`, so
/// tests drive it without a window, a session or the real defaults.
@MainActor
final class HomeModel: ObservableObject {
    /// What Home asks the app to do. AppDelegate fills these in.
    struct Actions {
        var newSticker: () -> Void = {}
        var replace: (UUID) -> Void = { _ in }
        var delete: (UUID) -> Void = { _ in }
        /// After the new shortcut is stored: re-register the hotkey, update
        /// the menu and re-render tutorial 1.
        var shortcutChanged: (Shortcut?) -> Void = { _ in }
        /// The recorder started (true) or stopped listening: suspend or resume the hotkey.
        var recordingChanged: (Bool) -> Void = { _ in }
        var menuBarIconChanged: (Bool) -> Void = { _ in }
        var sharingHiddenChanged: (Bool) -> Void = { _ in }
        /// The permission panel (spec section 14).
        var fixScreenRecording: () -> Void = {}
        /// Quit and reopen, in case the in-process Screen Recording check is stale.
        var relaunch: () -> Void = {}
        var quit: () -> Void = {}
    }

    /// A Delete from History waiting for its confirmation.
    struct PendingDelete: Equatable, Identifiable {
        let id: UUID
        let message: String
    }

    static let emptyHistoryText = "Stickers you make show up here. Click one to place it again."

    @Published private(set) var history: [StickerRecord] = []
    /// A library saved by a newer Stickermine: shown, never changed (spec section 12).
    @Published private(set) var isReadOnly = false
    /// A session runs: New Sticker and the grid are ignored (spec section 5.2).
    @Published var sessionActive = false
    @Published private(set) var shortcut: Shortcut?
    /// The shortcut is one of macOS's own enabled shortcuts.
    @Published private(set) var shortcutConflicts = false
    /// The last chord typed into the recorder had no ⌘ or ⌃.
    @Published private(set) var chordRejected = false
    @Published private(set) var showMenuBarIcon = true
    @Published private(set) var loginState: LoginItem.State = .on
    @Published private(set) var hideFromSharing = true
    @Published private(set) var screenRecordingOn = false
    /// Access was asked for once already (`didRequestScreenCapture`).
    @Published private(set) var screenRecordingRequested = false
    @Published var pendingDelete: PendingDelete?

    let library: LibraryController
    let thumbnails: ThumbnailCache
    let updater: Updater
    private let defaults: UserDefaults
    private let loginItem: LoginItem
    private let access: ScreenRecordingAccess
    private let conflicts: (Shortcut) -> Bool
    private let actions: Actions

    init(library: LibraryController, defaults: UserDefaults, loginItem: LoginItem, access: ScreenRecordingAccess,
         thumbnails: ThumbnailCache, updater: Updater,
         conflicts: @escaping (Shortcut) -> Bool = { SystemShortcuts.conflicts(with: $0) },
         actions: Actions) {
        self.library = library
        self.defaults = defaults
        self.loginItem = loginItem
        self.access = access
        self.thumbnails = thumbnails
        self.updater = updater
        self.conflicts = conflicts
        self.actions = actions
        refresh()
    }

    // MARK: Reading

    /// Re-reads everything: on creation and whenever Home becomes key, so a
    /// Screen Recording grant, a Login Items approval or a change made
    /// elsewhere shows at once (spec section 14).
    func refresh() {
        libraryChanged()
        shortcut = defaults.captureShortcut
        shortcutConflicts = shortcut.map(conflicts) ?? false
        showMenuBarIcon = defaults.showMenuBarIcon
        loginState = loginItem.state
        hideFromSharing = defaults.hideFromSharing
        screenRecordingOn = access.preflight()
        screenRecordingRequested = defaults.didRequestScreenCapture
    }

    /// The library changed (`LibraryController.onChange`).
    func libraryChanged() {
        isReadOnly = library.isReadOnly
        let items = HistoryList.items(in: library.library)
        if items != history { history = items }
        if let pending = pendingDelete, library.library.sticker(pending.id) == nil { pendingDelete = nil }
    }

    func pngURL(for sticker: UUID) -> URL {
        library.store.pngURL(for: sticker)
    }

    // MARK: History

    func newSticker() {
        guard !isReadOnly, !sessionActive else { return }
        actions.newSticker()
    }

    func replace(_ sticker: UUID) {
        guard !isReadOnly, !sessionActive else { return }
        actions.replace(sticker)
    }

    func requestDelete(_ sticker: UUID) {
        guard !isReadOnly, library.library.sticker(sticker) != nil else { return }
        pendingDelete = PendingDelete(
            id: sticker, message: HistoryList.deleteMessage(placementCount: library.library.placementCount(of: sticker)))
    }

    func confirmDelete() {
        guard let pending = pendingDelete else { return }
        pendingDelete = nil
        actions.delete(pending.id)
        libraryChanged()
    }

    func cancelDelete() {
        pendingDelete = nil
    }

    // MARK: Settings

    /// A recorded (or, with nil, cleared) shortcut.
    func setShortcut(_ shortcut: Shortcut?) {
        chordRejected = false
        defaults.captureShortcut = shortcut
        self.shortcut = shortcut
        shortcutConflicts = shortcut.map(conflicts) ?? false
        actions.shortcutChanged(shortcut)
    }

    func recordingChanged(_ recording: Bool) {
        if recording { chordRejected = false }
        actions.recordingChanged(recording)
    }

    func rejectChord() {
        chordRejected = true
    }

    func setShowMenuBarIcon(_ on: Bool) {
        defaults.showMenuBarIcon = on
        showMenuBarIcon = on
        actions.menuBarIconChanged(on)
    }

    func setLaunchAtLogin(_ on: Bool) {
        loginItem.setEnabled(on)
        loginState = loginItem.state
    }

    func openLoginItems() {
        loginItem.openSystemSettings()
    }

    func setHideFromSharing(_ on: Bool) {
        defaults.hideFromSharing = on
        hideFromSharing = on
        actions.sharingHiddenChanged(on)
    }

    func fixScreenRecording() {
        actions.fixScreenRecording()
    }

    func relaunch() {
        actions.relaunch()
    }

    func quit() {
        actions.quit()
    }
}
```

- Produces:

```text
struct HistoryGrid: View { @ObservedObject var model: HomeModel }       // in HistoryGrid.swift
enum SettingsText {                                                       // pure: every sentence the section shows
    static let shortcutTitle, shortcutHelp, voiceOverHelp, rejected, menuBarTitle, loginTitle, loginDetail,
               loginNeedsApproval, sharingTitle, sharingDetail, screenRecordingTitle: String
    static func conflict(_ shortcut: Shortcut) -> String; static func noShortcut(menuBarIcon: Bool) -> String
    static func menuBarDetail(on: Bool) -> String; static func screenRecordingDetail(on: Bool, requested: Bool) -> String
}
struct SettingsSection: View { @ObservedObject var model: HomeModel }
enum AboutLinks {                                                         // pure
    struct Row: Equatable, Identifiable { let title: String; let symbol: String; let help: String; let url: URL }
    static let website, repo, kofi, issues: URL; static let rows: [Row]  // Website, Star on GitHub, Buy me a coffee, Report a Bug
    static func pretty(_ url: URL) -> String; static func tooltip(for row: Row) -> String
}
enum VersionCheck: Equatable { case idle, checking, upToDate, available, failed
    static func line(version: String, check: VersionCheck) -> String }
struct AboutPopover: View { @ObservedObject var updater: Updater; let open: (URL) -> Void }
struct UpdateStrip: View { @ObservedObject var updater: Updater
    static let blue: Color; static func content(state: UpdateState, latest: String?) -> (text: String, showsButton: Bool)? }
struct HomeView: View { @ObservedObject var model: HomeModel; @ObservedObject var updater: Updater; let openURL: (URL) -> Void }
@MainActor final class HomeWindow: NSWindow { nonisolated static func isClose(_ event: NSEvent) -> Bool }   // ⌘W
@MainActor final class HomeWindowController: NSObject, NSWindowDelegate {
    static let initialSize: CGSize                                        // 640 × 640
    static let minimumSize: CGSize                                        // 560 × 480
    let window: HomeWindow; let model: HomeModel
    init(model: HomeModel, mainVisibleFrame: @escaping () -> CGRect?, openURL: @escaping (URL) -> Void = { NSWorkspace.shared.open($0) })
    static func makeWindow() -> HomeWindow; var isVisible: Bool { get }
    func show(); func close()
}
```

Home is an AppKit window (titled, closable, resizable, full-size content with a transparent titlebar, moved by its background, kept when closed, moved to the active Space when shown), hosting `HomeView` in an `NSHostingView` that only sets the window's minimum size. The window delegate is where the spec's key rules live: becoming key calls `model.refresh()` (Screen Recording, Login Items and the settings are read again, spec section 14), and resigning key or closing calls `ShortcutRecorderButton.active?.cancelRecording()` (Pullcord's `windowDidResignKey`/`windowWillClose`), so the hotkey always comes back.

```swift
    @Test func commandWClosesHomeAndNothingElseDoes() throws {

            try #require(NSEvent.keyEvent(with: .keyDown, location: .zero, modifierFlags: flags, timestamp: 0,
                                          windowNumber: 0, context: nil, characters: characters,
                                          charactersIgnoringModifiers: characters, isARepeat: false, keyCode: 13))
        }
        #expect(HomeWindow.isClose(try key("w", [.command])))
        // Caps Lock (and Fn) aren't modifiers here.
        #expect(HomeWindow.isClose(try key("w", [.command, .capsLock])))
        #expect(!HomeWindow.isClose(try key("w", [.command, .shift])))
        #expect(!HomeWindow.isClose(try key("w", [])))
        #expect(!HomeWindow.isClose(try key("q", [.command])))
    }

    @Test func resigningKeyOrClosingStopsTheRecorder() throws {
        let h = try HomeModelTests.harness()
        defer { h.finish() }
        let home = HomeWindowController(model: h.model, mainVisibleFrame: { nil })
        defer { home.window.close() }
        let recorder = ShortcutRecorderButton(shortcut: .defaultCapture)
        var states: [Bool] = []
        recorder.onRecordingChange = { states.append($0) }
        recorder.beginRecording()
        home.windowDidResignKey(Notification(name: NSWindow.didResignKeyNotification, object: home.window))
        #expect(!recorder.isRecording)
        recorder.beginRecording()
        home.windowWillClose(Notification(name: NSWindow.willCloseNotification, object: home.window))
        #expect(!recorder.isRecording)
        #expect(states == [true, false, true, false])
    }

    @Test func becomingKeyReadsTheSettingsAgain() throws {
        let h = try HomeModelTests.harness()
        defer { h.finish() }
        let home = HomeWindowController(model: h.model, mainVisibleFrame: { nil })
        defer { home.window.close() }
        h.access.granted = true
        h.defaults.showMenuBarIcon = false
        home.windowDidBecomeKey(Notification(name: NSWindow.didBecomeKeyNotification, object: home.window))
        #expect(h.model.screenRecordingOn)
        #expect(!h.model.showMenuBarIcon)
    }
}
```
