- [ ] **Step 3: Add the grid view**

In `Stickermine/Home/HistoryGrid.swift`, insert the following after the closing `}` of the declaration that starts with `final class ThumbnailCache {`, separated from it by one blank line:

```swift
/// The grid of every sticker ever made (spec section 13). A click re-places
/// one (the style-only picker, then placement); a right-click offers Delete
/// from History, which asks first.
struct HistoryGrid: View {
    @ObservedObject var model: HomeModel

    private let columns = [GridItem(.adaptive(minimum: 104, maximum: 136), spacing: 12)]

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Text("History")
                .font(.system(size: 15, weight: .semibold, design: .rounded))
            if model.history.isEmpty {
                Text(HomeModel.emptyHistoryText)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 96)
            } else {
                LazyVGrid(columns: columns, spacing: 12) {
                    ForEach(model.history) { record in
                        HistoryCell(record: record, url: model.pngURL(for: record.id), thumbnails: model.thumbnails,
                                    canEdit: !model.isReadOnly,
                                    onClick: { model.replace(record.id) },
                                    onDelete: { model.requestDelete(record.id) })
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

private struct HistoryCell: View {
    let record: StickerRecord
    let url: URL
    let thumbnails: ThumbnailCache
    let canEdit: Bool
    let onClick: () -> Void
    let onDelete: () -> Void

    @State private var image: CGImage?

    var body: some View {
        Button(action: onClick) {
            ZStack {
                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous)
                    .fill(Color.primary.opacity(0.05))
                if let image {
                    Image(decorative: image, scale: 1)
                        .resizable()
                        .interpolation(.high)
                        .scaledToFit()
                        .padding(10)
                } else {
                    ProgressView().controlSize(.small)
                }
            }
            .frame(height: 104)
            .contentShape(Rectangle())
        }
        .buttonStyle(.plain)
        .help(canEdit ? "Place this sticker again" : HUD.readOnlyMessage)
        .contextMenu {
            Button("Delete from History…", role: .destructive, action: onDelete)
                .disabled(!canEdit)
        }
        .task(id: record.id) {
            image = await thumbnails.thumbnail(for: record.id, at: url)
        }
    }
}
```

`Stickermine/Home/SettingsSection.swift` (complete file):

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// The words the settings section uses (spec section 13). Pure.
enum SettingsText {
    static let shortcutTitle = "Capture shortcut"
    static let shortcutHelp = "Click to record. Esc cancels, Delete clears. A shortcut needs ⌘ or ⌃ (F-keys can stand alone)."
    static let voiceOverHelp = "VoiceOver users: ⌃⌥⌘S is also VO-⌘-S, so you may want to record another shortcut."
    static let rejected = "That one needs ⌘ or ⌃ (F-keys can stand alone). Try another."
    static let menuBarTitle = "Show menu bar icon"
    static let loginTitle = "Launch at login"
    static let loginDetail = "Stickers only stay on your desktop while Stickermine runs."
    static let loginNeedsApproval = "macOS is waiting for your OK in System Settings › General › Login Items."
    static let sharingTitle = "Hide stickers from screen sharing and screenshots"
    static let sharingDetail = "Best effort: screen sharing, recording and screenshot apps usually leave stickers out. This also keeps them out of your own screenshots."
    static let screenRecordingTitle = "Screen Recording"

    /// The macOS-shortcut warning; Stickermine can't see other apps' shortcuts.
    static func conflict(_ shortcut: Shortcut) -> String {
        "\(shortcut.label) is also a macOS shortcut (System Settings › Keyboard › Keyboard Shortcuts), so it may not reach Stickermine."
    }

    /// What making a sticker takes without a shortcut (Review Focus 5: the
    /// way back to Home is always opening the app again).
    static func noShortcut(menuBarIcon: Bool) -> String {
        menuBarIcon
            ? "No shortcut: click the menu bar icon to make a sticker."
            : "No shortcut and no menu bar icon: open Stickermine again and click New Sticker."
    }

    static func menuBarDetail(on: Bool) -> String {
        on ? "Click it to make a sticker; right-click it for the menu."
            : "Without it, use the shortcut, or open Stickermine again to come back here."
    }

    /// Once access was asked for and is still off, the check may be stale
    /// (spec section 14), so Home also offers a relaunch.
    static func screenRecordingDetail(on: Bool, requested: Bool) -> String {
        if on { return "On. macOS will ask you to confirm it from time to time." }
        return requested
            ? "Off. If you just turned it on in System Settings, relaunch Stickermine so it sees the change."
            : "Off. macOS needs it before Stickermine can open the screenshot crosshair."
    }
}

/// Home's settings (spec section 13): the capture shortcut, the menu bar
/// icon, launch at login, hiding from screen sharing, and Screen Recording.
struct SettingsSection: View {
    @ObservedObject var model: HomeModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 16) {
            Text("Settings")
                .font(.system(size: 15, weight: .semibold, design: .rounded))
            shortcutRow
            toggle(SettingsText.menuBarTitle, isOn: model.showMenuBarIcon, set: model.setShowMenuBarIcon,
                   detail: SettingsText.menuBarDetail(on: model.showMenuBarIcon))
            loginRow
            toggle(SettingsText.sharingTitle, isOn: model.hideFromSharing, set: model.setHideFromSharing,
                   detail: SettingsText.sharingDetail)
            screenRecordingRow
        }
    }

    private var shortcutRow: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            HStack {
                Text(SettingsText.shortcutTitle)
                Spacer()
                ShortcutRecorder(shortcut: model.shortcut, onChange: model.setShortcut,
                                 onRecordingChange: model.recordingChanged, onRejected: model.rejectChord)
                    .frame(width: 170)
            }
            if model.chordRejected {
                detail(SettingsText.rejected, warning: true)
            } else if let shortcut = model.shortcut, model.shortcutConflicts {
                detail(SettingsText.conflict(shortcut), warning: true)
            } else if model.shortcut == nil {
                detail(SettingsText.noShortcut(menuBarIcon: model.showMenuBarIcon), warning: false)
            }
            detail(SettingsText.shortcutHelp, warning: false)
            detail(SettingsText.voiceOverHelp, warning: false)
        }
    }

    private var loginRow: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            Toggle(SettingsText.loginTitle, isOn: Binding(get: { model.loginState != .off },
                                                          set: { model.setLaunchAtLogin($0) }))
            if model.loginState == .requiresApproval {
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    detail(SettingsText.loginNeedsApproval, warning: true)
                    Button("Open Login Items") { model.openLoginItems() }
                        .controlSize(.small)
                }
            } else {
                detail(SettingsText.loginDetail, warning: false)
            }
        }
    }

    private var screenRecordingRow: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            HStack {
                Label(SettingsText.screenRecordingTitle,
                      systemImage: model.screenRecordingOn ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "exclamationmark.circle.fill")
                    .foregroundStyle(model.screenRecordingOn ? Color.green : Color.orange)
                Spacer()
                if !model.screenRecordingOn {
                    if model.screenRecordingRequested {
                        Button("Relaunch Stickermine") { model.relaunch() }
                    }
                    Button("Turn On…") { model.fixScreenRecording() }
                }
            }
            detail(SettingsText.screenRecordingDetail(on: model.screenRecordingOn,
                                                      requested: model.screenRecordingRequested), warning: false)
        }
    }

    private func toggle(_ title: String, isOn: Bool, set: @escaping (Bool) -> Void, detail text: String) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            Toggle(title, isOn: Binding(get: { isOn }, set: set))
            detail(text, warning: false)
        }
    }

    private func detail(_ text: String, warning: Bool) -> some View {
        Text(text)
            .font(.callout)
            .foregroundStyle(warning ? Color.orange : Color.secondary)
            .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
    }
}
```

```swift
import AppKit
import SwiftUI

/// The About popover's links, in the spec's order (section 13): Website,
/// Star on GitHub, Buy me a coffee, Report a Bug. The Website is the GitHub
/// Pages address until stickermine.app is bought and resolves (section 15).
enum AboutLinks {
    struct Row: Equatable, Identifiable {
        let title: String
        let symbol: String
        let help: String
        let url: URL
        var id: String { title }
    }

    static let website = URL(string: "https://grokcodile.github.io/stickermine/")!
    static let repo = URL(string: "https://github.com/grokcodile/stickermine")!
    static let kofi = URL(string: "https://ko-fi.com/grokcodile")!
    static let issues = URL(string: "https://github.com/grokcodile/stickermine/issues")!

    static let rows: [Row] = [
        Row(title: "Website", symbol: "globe", help: "Learn more about Stickermine", url: website),
        Row(title: "Star on GitHub", symbol: "star", help: "A star helps others find it", url: repo),
        Row(title: "Buy me a coffee", symbol: "mug", help: "Tips keep it going", url: kofi),
        Row(title: "Report a Bug", symbol: "ladybug", help: "Open an issue on GitHub", url: issues),
    ]

    /// The address as you'd say it: no scheme, no trailing slash.
    static func pretty(_ url: URL) -> String {
        var text = url.absoluteString
        for scheme in ["https://", "http://"] where text.hasPrefix(scheme) { text.removeFirst(scheme.count) }
        while text.hasSuffix("/") { text.removeLast() }
        return text
    }

    /// A row's tooltip: what it is for, then where it goes.
    static func tooltip(for row: Row) -> String {
        "\(row.help)\n↗ \(pretty(row.url))"
    }
}

/// The version line, which doubles as Check for Updates (Pullcord's).
enum VersionCheck: Equatable {
    case idle, checking, upToDate, available, failed

    static func line(version: String, check: VersionCheck) -> String {
        switch check {
        case .idle: return "Version \(version)"
        case .checking: return "Version \(version) · Checking…"
        case .upToDate: return "Version \(version) · Up to date"
        case .available: return "Version \(version) · Update available"
        case .failed: return "Version \(version) · Couldn’t check"
        }
    }
}

/// Hung from the ⓘ in Home's footer: icon, name, the version line (a click
/// checks for updates; Home's strip then offers the update) and the links.
struct AboutPopover: View {
    @ObservedObject var updater: Updater
    let open: (URL) -> Void

    @State private var check: VersionCheck = .idle

    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 10) {
            Image(nsImage: NSApp.applicationIconImage)
                .resizable()
                .frame(width: 64, height: 64)
            Text("Stickermine")
                .font(.system(size: 20, weight: .bold, design: .rounded))
            Button(VersionCheck.line(version: updater.appVersion, check: check)) { checkNow() }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .font(.system(size: 11))
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                .help("Check for updates")
            VStack(spacing: 6) {
                ForEach(AboutLinks.rows) { row in
                    Button { open(row.url) } label: {
                        Label(row.title, systemImage: row.symbol)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                    }
                    .controlSize(.large)
                    .help(AboutLinks.tooltip(for: row))
                }
            }
            .padding(.top, 6)
        }
        .padding(16)
        .frame(width: 268)
    }

    private func checkNow() {
        check = .checking
        updater.check { reachable in
            guard reachable else {
                check = .failed
                return
            }
            check = updater.state == .upToDate ? .upToDate : .available
        }
    }
}
```

`Stickermine/Home/UpdateStrip.swift` (complete file):

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// The band across the bottom of Home while an update is available, under
/// way or failed (spec section 13; Pullcord's strip, in the brand blue).
struct UpdateStrip: View {
    @ObservedObject var updater: Updater

    static let blue = Color(red: 0x3D / 255.0, green: 0x4B / 255.0, blue: 0xE0 / 255.0)

    /// What the strip says, and whether it offers Update; nil hides it.
    static func content(state: UpdateState, latest: String?) -> (text: String, showsButton: Bool)? {
        switch state {
        case .upToDate:
            return nil
        case .available:
            return (latest.map { "Update available: v\($0)" } ?? "Update available", true)
        case .downloading:
            return ("Downloading the update…", false)
        case .updating:
            return (latest.map { "Updating to v\($0)…" } ?? "Updating…", false)
        case .failed:
            return (latest.map { "The update to v\($0) didn’t finish" } ?? "The update didn’t finish", true)
        }
    }

    var body: some View {
        if let content = Self.content(state: updater.state, latest: updater.latestVersion) {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                Text(content.text)
                    .font(.system(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundStyle(.white)
                if content.showsButton {
                    Button { updater.performUpdate() } label: {
                        Text("Update")
                            .font(.system(size: 11, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundStyle(Self.blue)
                            .padding(.horizontal, 14)
                            .padding(.vertical, 3)
                            .background(Capsule().fill(Color.white.opacity(0.94)))
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .help(updater.isHomebrewManaged ? "Upgrade through Homebrew and reopen."
                                                    : "Download the new version and open it.")
                }
            }
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            .padding(.vertical, 9)
            .background(Self.blue)
        }
    }
}
```

```swift
import AppKit
import SwiftUI

/// Home's content (spec section 13): New Sticker at the top, the read-only
/// notice when the library came from a newer build, the history grid, the
/// settings, and a footer with the ⓘ About popover and Quit; the update
/// strip runs along the bottom while there is an update.
struct HomeView: View {
    @ObservedObject var model: HomeModel
    @ObservedObject var updater: Updater
    let openURL: (URL) -> Void

    @State private var showsAbout = false

    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 22) {
                    header
                    if model.isReadOnly { readOnlyNotice }
                    HistoryGrid(model: model)
                    Divider()
                    SettingsSection(model: model)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 24)
                // Room for the window buttons: the content runs under the transparent titlebar.
                .padding(.top, 34)
                .padding(.bottom, 20)
            }
            Divider()
            footer
            UpdateStrip(updater: updater)
        }
        .frame(minWidth: HomeWindowController.minimumSize.width, minHeight: HomeWindowController.minimumSize.height)
        .alert(HistoryList.deleteTitle,
               isPresented: Binding(get: { model.pendingDelete != nil }, set: { if !$0 { model.cancelDelete() } }),
               presenting: model.pendingDelete) { _ in
            Button("Delete", role: .destructive) { model.confirmDelete() }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) { model.cancelDelete() }
        } message: { pending in
            Text(pending.message)
        }
    }

    private var header: some View {
        HStack(alignment: .center) {
            Text("Stickermine")
                .font(.system(size: 26, weight: .semibold, design: .rounded))
            Spacer()
            Button { model.newSticker() } label: {
                Label("New Sticker", systemImage: "plus")
            }
            .controlSize(.large)
            .keyboardShortcut("n", modifiers: .command)
            .disabled(model.isReadOnly || model.sessionActive)
        }
    }

    private var readOnlyNotice: some View {
        Label(HUD.readOnlyMessage, systemImage: "lock.fill")
            .padding(10)
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous).fill(Color.orange.opacity(0.15)))
    }

    private var footer: some View {
        HStack {
            Button { showsAbout.toggle() } label: {
                Image(systemName: "info.circle")
                    .font(.system(size: 15))
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            .help("About Stickermine")
            .popover(isPresented: $showsAbout, arrowEdge: .top) {
                AboutPopover(updater: updater) { url in
                    showsAbout = false
                    openURL(url)
                }
            }
            Spacer()
            Button("Quit Stickermine") { model.quit() }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 20)
        .padding(.vertical, 12)
    }
}
```

`Stickermine/Home/HomeWindowController.swift` (complete file):

```swift
import AppKit
import SwiftUI

/// Home's window. ⌘W closes it: the app's main menu has no Close item.
@MainActor
final class HomeWindow: NSWindow {
    override func performKeyEquivalent(with event: NSEvent) -> Bool {
        if Self.isClose(event) {
            performClose(nil)
            return true
        }
        return super.performKeyEquivalent(with: event)
    }

    /// ⌘W and nothing else (Caps Lock, Fn and the keypad flag don't count).
    nonisolated static func isClose(_ event: NSEvent) -> Bool {
        event.type == .keyDown
            && event.modifierFlags.intersection([.command, .shift, .option, .control]) == .command
            && event.charactersIgnoringModifiers?.lowercased() == "w"
    }
}

/// The Home window (spec section 13), an AppKit window hosting `HomeView`.
/// Created once and kept: closing only orders it out. The first time it
/// shows, it is centred on the main display's visible frame at the size
/// `TutorialLayout.homeSize` gives (smaller on a small display), which is
/// where the first-run tutorial stickers keep clear of
/// (`TutorialLayout.homeFrame`); after that it stays wherever the user moved it. Becoming key re-reads the
/// settings and the Screen Recording state (spec section 14); resigning key
/// or closing stops the shortcut recorder, so the hotkey always comes back.
@MainActor
final class HomeWindowController: NSObject, NSWindowDelegate {
    static let initialSize = CGSize(width: 640, height: 640)
    static let minimumSize = CGSize(width: 560, height: 480)

    let window: HomeWindow
    let model: HomeModel
    private let mainVisibleFrame: () -> CGRect?
    private var hasShown = false

    init(model: HomeModel, mainVisibleFrame: @escaping () -> CGRect?,
         openURL: @escaping (URL) -> Void = { NSWorkspace.shared.open($0) }) {
        self.model = model
        self.mainVisibleFrame = mainVisibleFrame
        window = Self.makeWindow()
        super.init()
        let host = NSHostingView(rootView: HomeView(model: model, updater: model.updater, openURL: openURL))
        // SwiftUI sets only the minimum size; the window keeps the size it is
        // given, so its first frame is exactly the one the tutorials avoid.
        host.sizingOptions = [.minSize]
        window.contentView = host
        window.setContentSize(Self.initialSize)
        window.delegate = self
    }

    static func makeWindow() -> HomeWindow {
        let window = HomeWindow(contentRect: NSRect(origin: .zero, size: initialSize),
                                styleMask: [.titled, .closable, .resizable, .fullSizeContentView],
                                backing: .buffered, defer: false)
        window.title = "Stickermine"
        window.titleVisibility = .hidden
        window.titlebarAppearsTransparent = true
        window.isMovableByWindowBackground = true
        window.isReleasedWhenClosed = false
        window.isRestorable = false
        window.minSize = minimumSize
        // Opening Stickermine brings Home to the Space the user is on.
        window.collectionBehavior = [.moveToActiveSpace]
        return window
    }

    var isVisible: Bool { window.isVisible }

    /// Shows Home in front and activates Stickermine.
    func show() {
        if !hasShown {
            hasShown = true
            if let visible = mainVisibleFrame() {
                // Narrowed and shortened on a small display, so it fits and the
                // first-run tutorials' column beside it keeps its minimum width.
                let size = TutorialLayout.homeSize(preferred: Self.initialSize, minimum: Self.minimumSize, in: visible)
                window.setFrame(TutorialLayout.homeFrame(size: size, in: visible), display: false)
            } else {
                window.center()
            }
        }
        model.refresh()
        NSApp.activate()
        window.makeKeyAndOrderFront(nil)
        // NSApp.activate() is cooperative (macOS 14+): with no user input behind
        // it (the update check 6 s after a login launch) the system may decline,
        // and an inactive app's window would stay behind the active app's.
        window.orderFrontRegardless()
    }

    func close() {
        window.performClose(nil)
    }

    // MARK: NSWindowDelegate

    func windowDidBecomeKey(_ notification: Notification) {
        model.refresh()
    }

    func windowDidResignKey(_ notification: Notification) {
        ShortcutRecorderButton.active?.cancelRecording()
    }

    func windowWillClose(_ notification: Notification) {
        ShortcutRecorderButton.active?.cancelRecording()
        model.cancelDelete()
    }
}
```

### Task 14: AppDelegate: the launch, the hand-over, Home and the cleanup

**Files:**
- Modify: `Stickermine/App/AppDelegate.swift` (whole file), `Stickermine/App/StickermineApp.swift` (whole file), `Stickermine/App/SessionCoordinator.swift` (add `showPermissionPanel()`)
- Test: `StickermineTests/AppDelegateTests.swift` (whole file), `StickermineTests/SessionCoordinatorTests.swift` (one test added)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above, plus M2–M5's `LibraryController.onChange`, `StickerManager`, `SessionCoordinator` (`onActiveChange`, `onPlaced`, `startCapture(orderingOut:)`, `startReplace(sticker:)`, `isActive`, `cancel()`), `HotkeyCenter` (`register`, `suspend`, `resume`), `PermissionPanel.relaunch()`, `LiveScreenRecordingAccess`, `CaptureService.deleteLeftovers()`, `StyleGallery`.
- Produces:

```text
extension SessionCoordinator { func showPermissionPanel() }              // Home's Turn On…: the same panel a capture shows
extension AppDelegate {
    func applicationWillFinishLaunching(_ notification: Notification)    // the running copy is looked up
    func applicationShouldHandleReopen(_ sender: NSApplication, hasVisibleWindows flag: Bool) -> Bool   // opens Home
    func showHome(checkingForUpdate: Bool = true)                         // internal: the main menu's ⌘, calls it
}
```

What `AppDelegate` now does, in order: `applicationWillFinishLaunching` looks up a running copy (`SingleInstance.runningIncumbent()`). `applicationDidFinishLaunching` returns at once in the test host, sets `didRunStartup`, runs the style gallery instead when asked (before the hand-over: the gallery never touches the library, so `-styleGallery` works while the installed copy runs), reads the login flag, hands over and exits if a copy was found, sets the accessory policy, registers the defaults, then: `startStickers()` (M2–M5's restore, now reading `hideFromSharing` and `clickThroughFallback` through `Settings`, plus `TutorialStickers` and `library.onChange` → Home); `startCapture()` (the stored shortcut or none, the status item shown per `showMenuBarIcon` with Open Stickermine… and Quit, the session's activity going to the status item and Home, placement clicks to the support ask); `startShell(loginLaunch:)` (`LoginItem.applyAtLaunch()`, the `Updater` with the cleanup as `beforeHelper`, the `showHome` notification observer, then the `LaunchPlan`: tutorials on a first user launch, Home on every user launch, and after a login launch one update check 6 s later that opens Home only when it finds something). Home's actions: New Sticker → `startCapture(orderingOut: home.window)`; a grid click → `startReplace`; Delete → `HistoryDeletion.delete`; a new shortcut → `hotkeys.register`, `statusItem.setShortcut`, `tutorials.shortcutChanged`; recording → `hotkeys.suspend()`/`resume()`; the menu bar toggle → `statusItem.isVisible`; the sharing toggle → `setSharingHidden`; Turn On… → `session.showPermissionPanel()`; Relaunch → `PermissionPanel.relaunch()`; Quit → `quit()`. `applicationWillTerminate`, `quit()` and the updater's `beforeHelper` all run `terminationCleanup()`: cancel the session (terminating `screencapture` and deleting its file), dismiss the support ask, flush the library.

`Stickermine/App/AppDelegate.swift` (complete new version; it replaces the whole file):

```swift
import AppKit
import os

@MainActor
final class AppDelegate: NSObject, NSApplicationDelegate {
    /// True when this process is hosting the unit-test bundle. Every startup step
    /// (single-instance check, hotkey, status item, sticker restore, launch repair,
    /// SMAppService, updater) must be skipped then, so tests never show a window
    /// or touch system state (spec section 18).
    nonisolated static var isRunningTests: Bool {
        ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["XCTestConfigurationFilePath"] != nil
    }

    /// Set right after the test-host guard, so a test can prove the guard returned
    /// early.
    private(set) static var didRunStartup = false

    /// From design/spike-results.md, Decisions: "Alpha click-through works, or
    /// the fallback mode becomes the default?", kept in the pure `Settings`
    /// since M6 Task 1. The hidden `clickThroughFallback` setting (spec
    /// section 12) overrides it either way.
    nonisolated static let clickThroughFallbackByDefault = Settings.clickThroughFallbackByDefault

    private let log = Logger(subsystem: "com.ethan.stickermine", category: "app")
    private let defaults = UserDefaults.standard
    private var library: LibraryController?
    private var stickers: StickerManager?
    private var session: SessionCoordinator?
    private var screens: ScreenProvider?
    private var statusItem: StatusItemController?
    private var hotkeys: HotkeyCenter?
    private var loginItem: LoginItem?
    private var updater: Updater?
    private var tutorials: TutorialStickers?
    private var supportAsk: SupportAsk?
    private var home: HomeWindowController?
    private let thumbnails = ThumbnailCache()
    private var showHomeObserver: NSObjectProtocol?
    /// Another copy was already running when this one started (spec section 13).
    private var incumbent: NSRunningApplication?

    /// The single-instance check runs before anything can appear or register.
    /// Whether this is a login launch can only be read in
    /// `applicationDidFinishLaunching`, so the hand-over itself waits for that.
    func applicationWillFinishLaunching(_ notification: Notification) {
        guard !Self.isRunningTests else { return }
        incumbent = SingleInstance.runningIncumbent()
    }

    func applicationDidFinishLaunching(_ notification: Notification) {
        guard !Self.isRunningTests else { return }
        Self.didRunStartup = true
        if ProcessInfo.processInfo.arguments.contains(StyleGallery.launchArgument) {
            // Debug: render the style gallery and quit, without touching the
            // library. Before the hand-over, so it runs while another copy does.
            NSApp.setActivationPolicy(.accessory)
            StyleGallery.runAndQuit()
            return
        }
        let loginLaunch = SingleInstance.isLoginLaunch(NSAppleEventManager.shared().currentAppleEvent)
        if let incumbent {
            log.notice("another copy (pid \(incumbent.processIdentifier)) is running; handing over")
            SingleInstance.yield(to: incumbent, userLaunch: !loginLaunch)
        }
        // LSUIElement already makes this an agent app; saying so in code keeps it
        // true even for a launch whose Info.plist was not the one we built.
        NSApp.setActivationPolicy(.accessory)
        Settings.register(in: defaults)
        startStickers()
        startCapture()
        startShell(loginLaunch: loginLaunch)
    }

    /// SIGTERM skips this, so nothing may depend on it; the debounced save has
    /// usually run already.
    func applicationWillTerminate(_ notification: Notification) {
        terminationCleanup()
    }

    /// Opening the app again (Finder, Spotlight, the Dock) while it runs.
    func applicationShouldHandleReopen(_ sender: NSApplication, hasVisibleWindows flag: Bool) -> Bool {
        showHome()
        return true
    }

    func applicationSupportsSecureRestorableState(_ app: NSApplication) -> Bool {
        true
    }

    /// Loads the library (with launch repair) and restores every sticker on the
    /// connected displays. Every launch does this (spec section 13).
    private func startStickers() {
        let root: URL
        do {
            root = try LibraryStore.defaultRoot()
        } catch {
            log.error("no Application Support folder: \(error.localizedDescription, privacy: .public)")
            return
        }
        let store = LibraryStore(root: root)
        let library = LibraryController(store: store)
        let renderer = StyleRenderer(masterProvider: { id in MasterImage.image(at: store.pngURL(for: id)) })
        let screens = LiveScreenProvider()
        let stickers = StickerManager(library: library, screens: screens, renderer: renderer)
        stickers.setSharingHidden(defaults.hideFromSharing)
        if defaults.clickThroughFallback {
            stickers.enableClickThroughFallback()
        }
        stickers.startObservingDisplayChanges()
        stickers.restoreAll()
        renderer.prewarm()
        self.library = library
        self.stickers = stickers
        self.screens = screens
        log.notice("library \(Self.describe(library.loadResult), privacy: .public): \(library.library.stickers.count) stickers")

        let session = SessionCoordinator(capture: CaptureService(), cutout: CutoutEngine(), stickers: stickers,
                                         library: library, store: store, renderer: renderer, screens: screens)
        // Double-clicks don't enter editing while a session runs (spec section 5.2).
        stickers.isSessionActive = { [weak session] in session?.isActive ?? false }
        self.session = session
        tutorials = TutorialStickers(library: library, stickers: stickers, screens: screens, defaults: defaults)
        library.onChange = { [weak self] in self?.home?.model.libraryChanged() }
    }

    /// The capture triggers: the menu bar icon and the stored global shortcut
    /// (none once the user cleared it).
    private func startCapture() {
        // Leftovers from a run that was killed mid-capture (spec section 5.10).
        CaptureService.deleteLeftovers()
        guard let session else { return }
        let shortcut = defaults.captureShortcut
        let statusItem = StatusItemController(shortcut: shortcut)
        statusItem.isVisible = defaults.showMenuBarIcon
        statusItem.onCapture = { [weak session] in session?.startCapture() }
        statusItem.onOpenHome = { [weak self] in self?.showHome() }
        statusItem.onQuit = { [weak self] in self?.quit() }
        session.onActiveChange = { [weak self] active in self?.sessionActiveChanged(active) }
        session.onPlaced = { [weak self] _, sticker in self?.placementClicked(sticker) }
        let hotkeys = HotkeyCenter()
        hotkeys.register(shortcut) { [weak session] in session?.startCapture() }
        self.statusItem = statusItem
        self.hotkeys = hotkeys
        supportAsk = SupportAsk(defaults: defaults,
                                anchor: { [weak statusItem] in statusItem?.buttonScreenFrame },
                                fallbackArea: { [weak self] in self?.screens?.mainDisplay?.visibleFrame })
    }

    /// Launch at login, the updater, the hand-over notification and what this
    /// launch opens (spec section 13).
    private func startShell(loginLaunch: Bool) {
        let loginItem = LoginItem(defaults: defaults)
        loginItem.applyAtLaunch()
        self.loginItem = loginItem
        let updater = Updater(beforeHelper: { [weak self] in self?.terminationCleanup() })
        self.updater = updater
        showHomeObserver = DistributedNotificationCenter.default().addObserver(
            forName: SingleInstance.showHomeNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            MainActor.assumeIsolated { self?.showHome() }
        }
        guard let library else { return }
        let plan = LaunchPlan.decide(loginLaunch: loginLaunch, didFirstRun: defaults.didFirstRun,
                                     libraryIsEmpty: library.library.stickers.isEmpty, readOnly: library.isReadOnly)
        log.notice("launch: login=\(loginLaunch) home=\(plan.opensHome) tutorials=\(plan.placesTutorials)")
        if plan.placesTutorials {
            // The size Home's first show() gives it on this display, so the
            // tutorials keep clear of it there too.
            let homeSize = screens?.mainDisplay.map {
                TutorialLayout.homeSize(preferred: HomeWindowController.initialSize,
                                        minimum: HomeWindowController.minimumSize, in: $0.visibleFrame)
            } ?? HomeWindowController.initialSize
            tutorials?.placeFirstRun(shortcut: defaults.captureShortcut, homeSize: homeSize)
        }
        if plan.opensHome {
            showHome()
        }
        if plan.checksForUpdateAfterLogin {
            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + LaunchPlan.loginUpdateCheckDelay) { [weak self] in
                MainActor.assumeIsolated {
                    updater.check { [weak self] _ in
                        guard updater.state != .upToDate else { return }
                        self?.showHome(checkingForUpdate: false)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Home

    /// Opens Home (reopen, the hand-over notification, the menu's Open
    /// Stickermine…, ⌘,). Opening it checks for an update, unless the caller
    /// has just checked.
    func showHome(checkingForUpdate: Bool = true) {
        guard let home = home ?? makeHome() else { return }
        home.show()
        if checkingForUpdate { updater?.check() }
    }

    private func makeHome() -> HomeWindowController? {
        guard let library, let stickers, let session, let loginItem, let updater else { return nil }
        var actions = HomeModel.Actions()
        actions.newSticker = { [weak self, weak session] in session?.startCapture(orderingOut: self?.home?.window) }
        actions.replace = { [weak session] sticker in session?.startReplace(sticker: sticker) }
        actions.delete = { [weak self, weak session, weak stickers] sticker in
            guard let stickers else { return }
            HistoryDeletion.delete(sticker, session: session, stickers: stickers, thumbnails: self?.thumbnails)
        }
        actions.shortcutChanged = { [weak self] shortcut in self?.shortcutChanged(shortcut) }
        actions.recordingChanged = { [weak self] recording in
            if recording { self?.hotkeys?.suspend() } else { self?.hotkeys?.resume() }
        }
        actions.menuBarIconChanged = { [weak self] visible in self?.statusItem?.isVisible = visible }
        actions.sharingHiddenChanged = { [weak stickers] hidden in stickers?.setSharingHidden(hidden) }
        actions.fixScreenRecording = { [weak session] in session?.showPermissionPanel() }
        actions.relaunch = { PermissionPanel.relaunch() }
        actions.quit = { [weak self] in self?.quit() }
        let model = HomeModel(library: library, defaults: defaults, loginItem: loginItem,
                              access: LiveScreenRecordingAccess(), thumbnails: thumbnails, updater: updater,
                              actions: actions)
        model.sessionActive = session.isActive
        let home = HomeWindowController(model: model,
                                        mainVisibleFrame: { [weak self] in self?.screens?.mainDisplay?.visibleFrame })
        self.home = home
        return home
    }

    // MARK: Events

    private func sessionActiveChanged(_ active: Bool) {
        statusItem?.sessionActive = active
        home?.model.sessionActive = active
    }

    /// A placement click (fresh or re-placed); cross-display drags never come here.
    private func placementClicked(_ sticker: UUID) {
        let isTutorial = library?.library.sticker(sticker)?.tutorial ?? false
        supportAsk?.placementClicked(isTutorial: isTutorial)
    }

    /// Home stored the new shortcut: register it (or nothing), show it in the
    /// menu, and redraw tutorial 1 if it still exists.
    private func shortcutChanged(_ shortcut: Shortcut?) {
        hotkeys?.register(shortcut) { [weak session] in session?.startCapture() }
        statusItem?.setShortcut(shortcut)
        tutorials?.shortcutChanged(to: shortcut)
    }

    /// The menu's and Home's Quit: clean up, then quit (spec section 5.2).
    private func quit() {
        terminationCleanup()
        NSApp.terminate(nil)
    }

    /// Spec section 5.10, run by `applicationWillTerminate`, Quit and the
    /// updater before its helper: cancel the session (terminating a running
    /// screencapture and deleting its temporary file), decline a support ask
    /// that is up, and flush the pending library save.
    private func terminationCleanup() {
        session?.cancel()
        supportAsk?.dismiss()
        library?.flush()
    }

    private static func describe(_ result: LibraryStore.LoadResult) -> String {
        switch result {
        case .fresh: return "fresh"
        case .loaded: return "loaded"
        case .readOnlyNewer: return "read-only (saved by a newer Stickermine)"
        case .recoveredFromCorrupt: return "recovered from a corrupt library.json"
        }
    }
}
```

`Stickermine/App/StickermineApp.swift` (complete new version; it replaces the whole file):

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// The SwiftUI entry point. Its only scene is an empty Settings scene, so SwiftUI
/// never opens a window of its own: every window in Stickermine is an AppKit
/// window or panel owned by a controller that AppDelegate holds (spec section 4).
/// (`SwiftUI.Settings`, because the app's own `Settings` enum holds its
/// UserDefaults keys.)
///
/// The main menu is never shown (Stickermine is an agent app), but its key
/// equivalents work while Stickermine is active: ⌘, opens Home, which holds
/// the settings, instead of the empty Settings window, and there is no ⌘Q,
/// because quitting takes every sticker off the desktop and so is only ever
/// a deliberate click (Home's Quit, the menu bar menu's Quit), as in Key54.
/// Home closes itself on ⌘W (`HomeWindow`).
@main
struct StickermineApp: App {
    @NSApplicationDelegateAdaptor(AppDelegate.self) private var appDelegate

    var body: some Scene {
        SwiftUI.Settings {
            EmptyView()
        }
        .commands {
            CommandGroup(replacing: .appSettings) {
                Button("Settings…") { appDelegate.showHome() }
                    .keyboardShortcut(",", modifiers: .command)
            }
            CommandGroup(replacing: .appTermination) {}
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 15: The app icon

**Files:**
- Create: `design/make-icon.sh`, `Stickermine/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/Contents.json`, and the ten slices the script writes next to it (`icon_16x16.png` … `icon_512x512@2x.png`)
- Modify: `Stickermine.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` (`ASSETCATALOG_COMPILER_APPICON_NAME = AppIcon` in the app target's Debug and Release, as M1's notes asked)
- Test: `StickermineTests/AppIconTests.swift` (create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `design/icon-pickaxe.svg` (committed since M1; full-bleed: its rounded tile, radius 231, fills the 1024 canvas), `MasterImage.fileInfo(at:)` and `MasterImage.image(at:)`, `TestImages.rgba(of:x:y:)`, `SourceFiles` (test support).
- Produces: the `AppIcon` asset; Xcode's asset catalog compiler writes `AppIcon.icns` and `Assets.car` into the bundle and adds `CFBundleIconFile` and `CFBundleIconName` = `AppIcon` to the built Info.plist (checked with Xcode 27 beta while writing this plan; the root `Info.plist` stays untouched).

How the script works (each step checked while writing this plan on macOS 27.2): the SVG's root element becomes a nested 824 × 824 viewport at (100, 100) of a 1024 × 1024 canvas, so the tile sits on the 824/1024 grid like Pullcord's and Key54's (their 1024 slices are opaque from x = 100 to 923). `qlmanage -t -s 1024` renders it through WebKit, which keeps the pickaxe's drop shadow, but flattens the thumbnail onto an opaque page; so the script renders it twice, over white and over black, and recovers each pixel's alpha from the difference (over white a pixel shows a·C + (1 − a)·255, over black a·C) with a short Python step (`/usr/bin/python3 -I`, the standard library only). `sips -z` then scales the 1024 px master to every slice. The result: a 1024 slice whose opaque pixels span x and y 100–923, transparent corners and margins, and the tile's blue `#3D4BE0` at (150, 512).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the icon script**

`design/make-icon.sh` (complete file):

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Renders the app icon slices in Stickermine/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset
# from design/icon-pickaxe.svg (spec section 16). Run it again only when the
# SVG changes, then commit the PNGs: release.yml has no icon step.
#
# - The design SVG is full-bleed (its rounded tile fills the 1024 canvas). Mac
#   icons sit on the 824/1024 grid, like Pullcord's and Key54's: the tile is
#   drawn 824 pt wide, 100 pt in from every edge, on a transparent canvas.
# - qlmanage renders through WebKit, which keeps the SVG's drop shadow
#   (NSImage's SVG support drops it), but it flattens the thumbnail onto an
#   opaque page. So the icon is rendered twice, over white and over black,
#   and the alpha is recovered from the difference (alpha = 1 - (white - black)).
# - sips then scales the 1024 px master to every slice.
set -euo pipefail

cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
SVG="design/icon-pickaxe.svg"
SET="Stickermine/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset"
WORK="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "$WORK"' EXIT

# The design SVG's root element becomes a nested 824 x 824 viewport at (100, 100)
# of a 1024 x 1024 one, over a full-canvas backdrop.
fit() {
    echo '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 1024 1024" width="1024" height="1024">'
    echo "<rect width=\"1024\" height=\"1024\" fill=\"$1\"/>"
    sed '1s/<svg /<svg x="100" y="100" /; 1s/width="1024" height="1024"/width="824" height="824"/' "$SVG"
    echo '</svg>'
}
fit "#FFFFFF" > "$WORK/white.svg"
fit "#000000" > "$WORK/black.svg"
for backdrop in white black; do
    qlmanage -t -s 1024 -o "$WORK" "$WORK/$backdrop.svg" > /dev/null 2>&1
    if [ ! -s "$WORK/$backdrop.svg.png" ]; then
        echo "error: qlmanage didn't render $backdrop.svg" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
done

/usr/bin/python3 -I - "$WORK/white.svg.png" "$WORK/black.svg.png" "$WORK/icon_1024.png" <<'PY'
import struct, sys, zlib

def read_png(path):
    data = open(path, "rb").read()
    assert data[:8] == b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n", path
    pos, idat = 8, b""
    while pos < len(data):
        length, kind = struct.unpack(">I4s", data[pos:pos + 8])
        body = data[pos + 8:pos + 8 + length]
        if kind == b"IHDR":
            width, height, depth, color, _, _, interlace = struct.unpack(">IIBBBBB", body)
            assert depth == 8 and color in (2, 6) and interlace == 0, (path, depth, color, interlace)
        elif kind == b"IDAT":
            idat += body
        pos += 12 + length
    channels = 4 if color == 6 else 3
    raw = zlib.decompress(idat)
    stride = width * channels
    rows, prev = [], bytearray(stride)
    for y in range(height):
        start = y * (stride + 1)
        kind, line = raw[start], bytearray(raw[start + 1:start + 1 + stride])
        for x in range(stride):
            left = line[x - channels] if x >= channels else 0
            up = prev[x]
            corner = prev[x - channels] if x >= channels else 0
            if kind == 1:
                line[x] = (line[x] + left) & 255
            elif kind == 2:
                line[x] = (line[x] + up) & 255
            elif kind == 3:
                line[x] = (line[x] + ((left + up) >> 1)) & 255
            elif kind == 4:
                p = left + up - corner
                pa, pb, pc = abs(p - left), abs(p - up), abs(p - corner)
                line[x] = (line[x] + (left if pa <= pb and pa <= pc else up if pb <= pc else corner)) & 255
        rows.append(line)
        prev = line
    return width, height, channels, rows

def write_png(path, width, height, rows):
    def chunk(kind, body):
        return struct.pack(">I", len(body)) + kind + body + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(kind + body) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
    raw = b"".join(b"\x00" + bytes(row) for row in rows)
    with open(path, "wb") as out:
        out.write(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")
        out.write(chunk(b"IHDR", struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", width, height, 8, 6, 0, 0, 0)))
        out.write(chunk(b"sRGB", b"\x00"))
        out.write(chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(raw, 9)))
        out.write(chunk(b"IEND", b""))

w, h, cw, white = read_png(sys.argv[1])
w2, h2, cb, black = read_png(sys.argv[2])
assert (w, h) == (w2, h2) == (1024, 1024), (w, h, w2, h2)
rows = []
for y in range(h):
    row = bytearray(w * 4)
    for x in range(w):
        on_white = white[y][x * cw:x * cw + 3]
        on_black = black[y][x * cb:x * cb + 3]
        # Over white a pixel shows a*C + (1 - a)*255, over black a*C.
        alpha = 255 - sum(on_white[i] - on_black[i] for i in range(3)) / 3
        alpha = max(0, min(255, round(alpha)))
        if alpha > 0:
            for i in range(3):
                row[x * 4 + i] = max(0, min(255, round(on_black[i] * 255 / alpha)))
        row[x * 4 + 3] = alpha
    rows.append(row)
write_png(sys.argv[3], w, h, rows)
PY

mkdir -p "$SET"
for size in 16 32 128 256 512; do
    double=$((size * 2))
    sips -z "$size" "$size" "$WORK/icon_1024.png" --out "$SET/icon_${size}x${size}.png" > /dev/null
    if [ "$double" -eq 1024 ]; then
        cp "$WORK/icon_1024.png" "$SET/icon_${size}x${size}@2x.png"
    else
        sips -z "$double" "$double" "$WORK/icon_1024.png" --out "$SET/icon_${size}x${size}@2x.png" > /dev/null
    fi
done
ls "$SET"/icon_*.png | while read -r slice; do
    echo "$(basename "$slice") $(sips -g pixelWidth "$slice" | awk '/pixelWidth/ { print $2 }') px"
done
```

````markdown
# App shell checks (M6)

M6's app shell (spec sections 13, 12, 14 and 16) needs a person at the Mac: real launches from Finder and at login, a second copy, System Settings, the menu bar, focus between real apps, screenshots, screen sharing and recordings. An agent executing the plan asks Ethan to do each action and writes down what he reports and the named log lines; it never fills in a Result itself. Rows (or, in row 15b, apps) that need something that isn't there (a second Mac, a Mac on macOS 26.x, an AirPlay receiver or iPad, a published release) get `not run: <reason>`.

## Setup

Run these in bash or zsh, not fish.

1. Keep the real library and settings out of the way (the checks start from a first run and change every setting). Run this block once; to reinstall after a fix, run only `./install.sh`:

   ```bash
   LIB="$HOME/Library/Application Support/com.ethan.stickermine"
   pkill -x Stickermine || true
   if [ -e "$LIB" ] && [ ! -e "$LIB.before-m6" ]; then mv "$LIB" "$LIB.before-m6"; fi
   [ -e "$HOME/stickermine-defaults-before-m6.plist" ] || defaults export com.ethan.stickermine "$HOME/stickermine-defaults-before-m6.plist" 2>/dev/null || true
   defaults delete com.ethan.stickermine 2>/dev/null || true
   ```

2. `./install.sh`. It must print `Signing with: DEA1A3749B06CF3619F72152EFC35A48E380C4E4` and `Running instances: 1`. It launches `/Applications/Stickermine.app` with `open` (a user launch, so this is the first run).
3. In a second terminal: `log stream --style compact --predicate 'subsystem == "com.ethan.stickermine"'`.
4. In the first terminal, define two helpers (the library is saved 0.5 s after a change: wait a second before using them):

   ```bash
   LIB="$HOME/Library/Application Support/com.ethan.stickermine"
   counts() { python3 -c 'import json,sys; d=json.load(open(sys.argv[1])); print("stickers", len(d["stickers"]), "tutorial", sum(1 for s in d["stickers"] if s.get("tutorial")), "placements", sum(len(r["placements"]) for r in d["displays"].values()))' "$LIB/library.json"; }
   setting() { defaults read com.ethan.stickermine "$1" 2>/dev/null || echo "(default)"; }
   ```
````

For a failed row: reproduce it in a unit test where the logic allows (`Settings`, `ShortcutRecording`, `SystemShortcuts`, `LaunchPlan`, `SingleInstance.incumbent`, `TutorialLayout`, `SupportAskRule`, `SupportAsk.frame`, `HistoryList`, a `HomeModel` with recorded actions, `TutorialStickers` against an `EditingHarness`), fix it, run the whole suite, `./install.sh`, repeat the row, and write the fix into the Outcome section. Known fallbacks: if row 14's login launch opens Home, log `launch: login=…` shows what `SingleInstance.isLoginLaunch` read: record it, then look at `NSAppleEventManager.shared().currentAppleEvent` in a log line in `applicationDidFinishLaunching` (the event Pullcord's check relies on) before changing the rule. If row 20's card takes focus from Safari, check in the log that nothing calls `makeKey` on it; the panel must only ever be ordered in with `orderFrontRegardless()`. If row 13 shows "waiting for your OK" on every launch, record it: that is macOS's state, which Home only reports. If row 26's Home opens behind the Finder window after the login, check that `HomeWindowController.show()` still ends with `window.orderFrontRegardless()` (macOS may turn down `NSApp.activate()` when the last input went to another app). Row 15b is best-effort (spec section 13: hiding from screen sharing is described as best-effort): an app whose share or recording shows a sticker with the setting on is written down in Result and in Outcome, not fixed; a sticker that stays out of an app's image with the setting off, or that changes on screen, is a failure to fix like any other. If every row passed, write `All rows passed.` in Outcome.

- [ ] **Step 5: CI dry run (needs Ethan's explicit yes now)**

If `git remote get-url origin` fails, the GitHub repo doesn't exist yet: append the "not run" row and stop here (go to Step 6):

```bash
REASON="no GitHub repo yet"
echo "| $(date +%F) | M6 end (Task 17) | $(git rev-parse --short HEAD) | none | not run | Ethan: $REASON |" >> design/ci-dry-runs.md
```

Otherwise ask Ethan: "Push main to grokcodile/stickermine and start a release-workflow dry run?" Only after his yes:

```bash
git push
BEFORE=$(gh run list --workflow release.yml --branch main --event workflow_dispatch --limit 1 --json databaseId --jq '.[0].databaseId // 0')
gh workflow run release.yml --ref main
RUN_ID="$BEFORE"
for _ in $(seq 30); do
  sleep 2
  RUN_ID=$(gh run list --workflow release.yml --branch main --event workflow_dispatch --limit 1 --json databaseId --jq '.[0].databaseId // 0')
  [ "$RUN_ID" != "$BEFORE" ] && break
done
if [ "$RUN_ID" = "$BEFORE" ]; then
  echo "error: the dispatched run never appeared" >&2
else
  gh run watch "$RUN_ID" --exit-status
  ROW=$(gh run view "$RUN_ID" --json headSha,url,conclusion --jq '"\(.headSha[0:7]) | \(.url) | \(.conclusion)"')
  echo "| $(date +%F) | M6 end (Task 17) | ${ROW} | |" >> design/ci-dry-runs.md
fi
```

Expected: `gh run watch` exits 0 and the appended row ends `| success | |`. If Ethan says no, append the "not run" row above with his reason in `REASON`. If it prints `error: the dispatched run never appeared`, nothing was recorded: check `gh run list --workflow release.yml` and rerun from `BEFORE=`. On a failed run, read `gh run view "$RUN_ID" --log-failed`. An unknown-symbol error means a macOS 27-only API slipped in (fix it, run the suite, commit, and ask before pushing again); an `actool` error about `AppIcon` means CI's Xcode 26.6 rejects the icon set (check `Contents.json` against Task 15 and that all ten PNGs are committed); a project-format error means the project file changed from objectVersion 77.

## Notes for later milestones

- **M7** (README, PRIVACY, TERMS, site): the README's First run section describes the three tutorial stickers and that they go away for good when removed; its settings list follows `SettingsText` (the shortcut needs ⌘ or ⌃, the VoiceOver note, hiding from screen sharing being best-effort and also hiding stickers from your own screenshots); PRIVACY names the update check (`Updater.latestReleaseURL`, a GET with nothing sent, only on Home open, the About version line and once after a login launch) as the only network request, and Ko-fi as the support ask's and the About popover's only other destination. `docs/icon.png` is exported from `Stickermine/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/icon_256x256@2x.png` (512 px on the 824 grid). Once `stickermine.app` resolves, `AboutLinks.website` changes to it in the same commit as `docs/CNAME`. Before the first tag, keep `Info.plist`'s version ahead of it (the updater compares against it), and run `design/app-shell-checks.md` row 26 against the published release.
